    18→
    19→// TestShadow_FullWorkflow tests the complete shadow workflow as described in
    20→// docs/requirements/shadow-strategy/example.md
    21→//
    22→// This test simulates Alice's workflow:
    23→// 1. Start session, create checkpoints
    24→// 2. Rewind to earlier checkpoint
    25→// 3. Create new checkpoint after rewind
    26→// 4. User commits (triggers condensation)
    27→// 5. Continue working after commit (new shadow branch)
    28→// 6. User commits again (second condensation)
    29→// 7. Verify final state
    30→func TestShadow_FullWorkflow(t *testing.T) {
    31→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    32→	defer env.Cleanup()
    33→
    34→	// ========================================
    35→	// Phase 1: Setup
    36→	// ========================================
    37→	env.InitRepo()
    38→
    39→	// Create initial commit on main
    40→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
    41→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    42→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    43→
    44→	// Switch to feature branch (shadow skips main/master)
    45→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/auth")
    46→
    47→	// Initialize Entire AFTER branch switch to avoid go-git cleaning untracked files
    48→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    49→
    50→	initialHead := env.GetHeadHash()
    51→	t.Logf("Initial HEAD on feature/auth: %s", initialHead[:7])
    52→
    53→	// ========================================
    54→	// Phase 2: Session Start & First Checkpoint
    55→	// ========================================
    56→	t.Log("Phase 2: Starting session and creating first checkpoint")
    57→
    58→	session := env.NewSession()
    59→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
    60→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	// Verify session state file exists in .git/entire-sessions/
    64→	sessionStateDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
    65→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessionStateDir)
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session state dir: %v", err)
    68→	}
    69→	if len(entries) == 0 {
    70→		t.Error("Expected session state file in .git/entire-sessions/")
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// Create first file (src/auth.go)
    74→	authV1 := "package auth\n\nfunc Authenticate(user, pass string) bool {\n\treturn false\n}"
    75→	env.WriteFile("src/auth.go", authV1)
    76→
    77→	session.CreateTranscript(
    78→		"Create authentication module",
    79→		[]FileChange{{Path: "src/auth.go", Content: authV1}},
    80→	)
    81→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    82→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	// Verify shadow branch created with correct worktree-specific naming
    86→	expectedShadowBranch := env.GetShadowBranchNameForCommit(initialHead)
    87→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch) {
    88→		t.Errorf("Expected shadow branch %s to exist", expectedShadowBranch)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Verify 1 rewind point
    92→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
    93→	if len(points) != 1 {
    94→		t.Fatalf("Expected 1 rewind point after first checkpoint, got %d", len(points))
    95→	}
    96→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1 created: %s", points[0].Message)
    97→
    98→	// ========================================
    99→	// Phase 3: Second Checkpoint (continuing same session)
   100→	// ========================================
   101→	t.Log("Phase 3: Creating second checkpoint (continuing same session)")
   102→
   103→	// Continue the same session (not a new session) - this is the expected Claude behavior
   104→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   105→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Create hash.go and modify auth.go
   109→	hashV1 := "package auth\n\nimport \"crypto/sha256\"\n\nfunc HashPassword(pass string) []byte {\n\th := sha256.Sum256([]byte(pass))\n\treturn h[:]\n}"
   110→	authV2 := "package auth\n\nfunc Authenticate(user, pass string) bool {\n\t// TODO: use hashed passwords\n\treturn false\n}"
   111→	env.WriteFile("src/hash.go", hashV1)
   112→	env.WriteFile("src/auth.go", authV2)
   113→
   114→	// Reset transcript builder for the new checkpoint
   115→	session.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   116→	session.CreateTranscript(
   117→		"Add password hashing",
   118→		[]FileChange{
   119→			{Path: "src/hash.go", Content: hashV1},
   120→			{Path: "src/auth.go", Content: authV2},
   121→		},
   122→	)
   123→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   124→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	// Verify 2 rewind points on same shadow branch
   128→	points = env.GetRewindPoints()
   129→	if len(points) != 2 {
   130→		t.Fatalf("Expected 2 rewind points after second checkpoint, got %d", len(points))
   131→	}
   132→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2 created: %s", points[0].Message)
   133→
   134→	// Verify both files exist
   135→	if !env.FileExists("src/hash.go") {
   136→		t.Error("src/hash.go should exist after checkpoint 2")
   137→	}
   138→	if content := env.ReadFile("src/auth.go"); content != authV2 {
   139→		t.Errorf("src/auth.go should have v2 content, got: %s", content)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// ========================================
   143→	// Phase 4: Rewind to First Checkpoint
   144→	// ========================================
   145→	t.Log("Phase 4: Rewinding to first checkpoint")
   146→
   147→	// Find checkpoint 1 by message (the one for "Create authentication module")
   148→	var checkpoint1ID string
   149→	for _, p := range points {
   150→		if p.Message == "Create authentication module" {
   151→			checkpoint1ID = p.ID
   152→			break
   153→		}
   154→	}
   155→	if checkpoint1ID == "" {
   156→		t.Fatalf("Could not find checkpoint for 'Create authentication module' in %d points", len(points))
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1ID); err != nil {
   160→		t.Fatalf("Rewind to checkpoint 1 failed: %v", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	// Verify hash.go was removed (it was only added in checkpoint 2)
   164→	if env.FileExists("src/hash.go") {
   165→		t.Error("src/hash.go should NOT exist after rewind to checkpoint 1")
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	// Verify auth.go restored to v1 (without the "TODO: use hashed passwords" comment)
   169→	content := env.ReadFile("src/auth.go")
   170→	if content != authV1 {
   171→		t.Errorf("src/auth.go should be restored to v1 after rewind, got: %s", content)
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	// Verify HEAD unchanged (shadow doesn't modify user's branch)
   175→	if head := env.GetHeadHash(); head != initialHead {
   176→		t.Errorf("HEAD should be unchanged after rewind, got %s, want %s", head[:7], initialHead[:7])
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Verify shadow branch still exists (history preserved)
   180→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch) {
   181→		t.Errorf("Shadow branch %s should still exist after rewind", expectedShadowBranch)
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	// ========================================
   185→	// Phase 5: New Checkpoint After Rewind (continue same session)
   186→	// ========================================
   187→	t.Log("Phase 5: Creating checkpoint after rewind (continuing same session)")
   188→
   189→	// Continue the same session after rewind
   190→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   191→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (after rewind) failed: %v", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Reset transcript builder for next checkpoint
   195→	session.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   196→
   197→	// Create bcrypt.go instead (different approach)
   198→	bcryptV1 := "package auth\n\nimport \"golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt\"\n\nfunc HashPassword(pass string) ([]byte, error) {\n\treturn bcrypt.GenerateFromPassword([]byte(pass), bcrypt.DefaultCost)\n}"
   199→	authV3 := "package auth\n\nfunc Authenticate(user, pass string) bool {\n\t// Use bcrypt for password hashing\n\treturn false\n}"
   200→	env.WriteFile("src/bcrypt.go", bcryptV1)
   201→	env.WriteFile("src/auth.go", authV3)
   202→
   203→	session.CreateTranscript(
   204→		"Use bcrypt for hashing",
   205→		[]FileChange{
   206→			{Path: "src/bcrypt.go", Content: bcryptV1},
   207→			{Path: "src/auth.go", Content: authV3},
   208→		},
   209→	)
   210→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   211→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 3) failed: %v", err)
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Verify we now have checkpoints (may be 3 if history preserved, or could vary)
   215→	points = env.GetRewindPoints()
   216→	if len(points) < 1 {
   217→		t.Fatalf("Expected at least 1 rewind point after checkpoint 3, got %d", len(points))
   218→	}
   219→	t.Logf("After rewind and new checkpoint: %d rewind points", len(points))
   220→
   221→	// ========================================
   222→	// Phase 6: User Commits (Condensation)
   223→	// ========================================
   224→	t.Log("Phase 6: User commits - triggering condensation")
   225→
   226→	// Stage and commit with shadow hooks
   227→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add user authentication with bcrypt", "src/auth.go", "src/bcrypt.go")
   228→
   229→	// Get the new commit
   230→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   231→	t.Logf("User commit 1: %s", commit1Hash[:7])
   232→
   233→	// Active branch commits should be clean (no Entire-* trailers)
   234→	commitMsg := env.GetCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
   235→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg, "Entire-Session:") {
   236→		t.Errorf("Commit should NOT have Entire-Session trailer (clean history), got: %s", commitMsg)
   237→	}
   238→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg, "Entire-Condensation:") {
   239→		t.Errorf("Commit should NOT have Entire-Condensation trailer (clean history), got: %s", commitMsg)
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Get checkpoint ID by walking history - verifies condensation added the trailer
   243→	checkpoint1ID = env.GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory()
   244→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1 ID: %s", checkpoint1ID)
   245→
   246→	// Verify entire/sessions branch exists with checkpoint folder
   247→	if !env.BranchExists(paths.MetadataBranchName) {
   248→		t.Error("entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   249→	}
   250→
   251→	// Verify checkpoint folder contents (check via git show)
   252→	// Uses sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/metadata.json
   253→	checkpointPath := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpoint1ID) + "/metadata.json"
   254→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointPath) {
   255→		t.Errorf("Checkpoint folder should contain metadata.json at %s", checkpointPath)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Clear session state to simulate session completion (avoids concurrent session warning)
   259→	if err := env.ClearSessionState(session.ID); err != nil {
   260→		t.Fatalf("ClearSessionState failed: %v", err)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	// ========================================
   264→	// Phase 7: Continue Working After Commit
   265→	// ========================================
   266→	t.Log("Phase 7: Continuing work after user commit")
   267→
   268→	// Verify HEAD changed
   269→	if commit1Hash == initialHead {
   270→		t.Error("HEAD should have changed after user commit")
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	// Start new session
   274→	session4 := env.NewSession()
   275→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session4.ID); err != nil {
   276→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (session4) failed: %v", err)
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	// Create session management file
   280→	sessionMgmt := "package auth\n\nimport \"time\"\n\ntype Session struct {\n\tUserID string\n\tExpiry time.Time\n}"
   281→	env.WriteFile("src/session.go", sessionMgmt)
   282→
   283→	session4.CreateTranscript(
   284→		"Add session management",
   285→		[]FileChange{{Path: "src/session.go", Content: sessionMgmt}},
   286→	)
   287→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session4.ID, session4.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   288→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 4) failed: %v", err)
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Verify NEW shadow branch created (based on new HEAD)
   292→	expectedShadowBranch2 := env.GetShadowBranchNameForCommit(commit1Hash)
   293→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch2) {
   294→		t.Errorf("Expected new shadow branch %s after commit", expectedShadowBranch2)
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	// Verify it's different from the first shadow branch
   298→	if expectedShadowBranch == expectedShadowBranch2 {
   299→		t.Error("New shadow branch should have different name than first (different base commit)")
   300→	}
   301→	t.Logf("New shadow branch after commit: %s", expectedShadowBranch2)
   302→
   303→	// ========================================
   304→	// Phase 8: Second User Commit
   305→	// ========================================
   306→	t.Log("Phase 8: Second user commit")
   307→
   308→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add session management", "src/session.go")
   309→
   310→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   311→	t.Logf("User commit 2: %s", commit2Hash[:7])
   312→
   313→	// Verify commit is clean (no trailers)
   314→	commitMsg2 := env.GetCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
   315→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg2, "Entire-Session:") {
   316→		t.Errorf("Commit should NOT have Entire-Session trailer (clean history), got: %s", commitMsg2)
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	// Get checkpoint ID from commit message trailer (not from timestamp-based matching
   320→	// which is flaky when commits happen within the same second)
   321→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
   322→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2 ID: %s", checkpoint2ID)
   323→
   324→	// Verify DIFFERENT checkpoint ID
   325→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint2ID {
   326→		t.Errorf("Second commit should have different checkpoint ID: %s vs %s", checkpoint1ID, checkpoint2ID)
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	// Verify second checkpoint folder exists (uses sharded path)
   330→	checkpoint2Path := ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpoint2ID) + "/metadata.json"
   331→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpoint2Path) {
   332→		t.Errorf("Second checkpoint folder should exist at %s", checkpoint2Path)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	// ========================================
   336→	// Phase 9: Verify Final State
   337→	// ========================================
   338→	t.Log("Phase 9: Verifying final state")
   339→
   340→	// 2 user commits on feature branch
   341→	// Both should be clean (no Entire-* trailers)
   342→	if strings.Contains(commitMsg, "Entire-Session:") || strings.Contains(commitMsg2, "Entire-Session:") {
   343→		t.Error("Commits should NOT have Entire-Session trailer (clean history)")
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	// 2 checkpoint folders in entire/sessions (Already verified above)
   347→
   348→	// Verify all expected files exist in working directory
   349→	expectedFiles := []string{"README.md", "src/auth.go", "src/bcrypt.go", "src/session.go"}
   350→	for _, f := range expectedFiles {
   351→		if !env.FileExists(f) {
   352→			t.Errorf("Expected file %s to exist in final state", f)
   353→		}
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	// Verify shadow branches exist (can be pruned later)
   357→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch) {
   358→		t.Logf("Note: First shadow branch %s may have been cleaned up", expectedShadowBranch)
   359→	}
   360→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedShadowBranch2) {
   361→		t.Logf("Note: Second shadow branch %s may have been cleaned up", expectedShadowBranch2)
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	t.Log("Shadow full workflow test completed successfully!")
   365→}
   366→
   367→// TestShadow_SessionStateLocation verifies session state is stored in .git/
   368→// (not .entire/) so it's never accidentally committed.
   369→func TestShadow_SessionStateLocation(t *testing.T) {
   370→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   371→	defer env.Cleanup()
   372→
   373→	env.InitRepo()
   374→
   375→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
   376→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   377→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   378→
   379→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   380→
   381→	// Initialize AFTER branch switch
   382→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   383→
   384→	session := env.NewSession()
   385→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   386→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	// Session state should be in .git/entire-sessions/, NOT .entire/
   390→	gitSessionDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
   391→	entireSessionDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", "entire-sessions")
   392→
   393→	if _, err := os.Stat(gitSessionDir); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   394→		t.Error("Session state directory should exist at .git/entire-sessions/")
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireSessionDir); err == nil {
   398→		t.Error("Session state should NOT be in .entire/entire-sessions/")
   399→	}
   400→}
   401→
   402→// TestShadow_MultipleConcurrentSessions tests that starting a second Claude session
   403→// while another session has uncommitted checkpoints triggers a warning.
   404→// The first prompt is blocked with continue:false, subsequent prompts proceed.
   405→func TestShadow_MultipleConcurrentSessions(t *testing.T) {
   406→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   407→	defer env.Cleanup()
   408→
   409→	env.InitRepo()
   410→
   411→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
   412→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   413→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   414→
   415→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   416→
   417→	// Initialize AFTER branch switch
   418→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   419→
   420→	// Start first session
   421→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   422→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   423→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (session1) failed: %v", err)
   424→	}
   425→
   426→	// Create a checkpoint for session1 (this creates the shadow branch)
   427→	env.WriteFile("file.txt", "content")
   428→	session1.CreateTranscript("Add file", []FileChange{{Path: "file.txt", Content: "content"}})
   429→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   430→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (session1) failed: %v", err)
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	// Verify session state file exists
   434→	sessionStateDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
   435→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessionStateDir)
   436→	if err != nil {
   437→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session state dir: %v", err)
   438→	}
   439→	if len(entries) != 1 {
   440→		t.Errorf("Expected 1 session state file, got %d", len(entries))
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	// Starting a second session while session1 has uncommitted checkpoints triggers warning
   444→	// The hook outputs JSON {"continue":false,...} but returns nil (success)
   445→	session2 := env.NewSession()
   446→	err = env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID)
   447→	// The hook succeeds (returns nil) but outputs JSON with continue:false
   448→	// The test infrastructure treats this as success since the hook didn't return an error
   449→	if err != nil {
   450→		t.Logf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit returned error (expected in some cases): %v", err)
   451→	}
   452→
   453→	// Verify session2 state file was created with ConcurrentWarningShown flag
   454→	// This is set by the hook when it outputs continue:false
   455→	entries, err = os.ReadDir(sessionStateDir)
   456→	if err != nil {
   457→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session state dir: %v", err)
   458→	}
   459→	if len(entries) != 2 {
   460→		t.Errorf("Expected 2 session state files after second session attempt, got %d", len(entries))
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// Clear session1 state file - this makes the shadow branch "orphaned"
   464→	if err := env.ClearSessionState(session1.ID); err != nil {
   465→		t.Fatalf("ClearSessionState failed: %v", err)
   466→	}
   467→
   468→	// Session2 had ConcurrentWarningShown=true, but now the conflict is resolved
   469→	// (session1 state cleared), so the warning flag is cleared and hooks proceed normally.
   470→	// The orphaned shadow branch (from session1) is reset, allowing session2 to proceed.
   471→	err = env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session2.ID)
   472→	if err != nil {
   473→		t.Errorf("Expected success after orphaned shadow branch is reset, got: %v", err)
   474→	} else {
   475→		t.Log("Session2 proceeded after orphaned shadow branch was reset")
   476→	}
   477→}
   478→
   479→// TestShadow_ShadowBranchMigrationOnPull verifies that when the base commit changes
   480→// (e.g., after stash → pull → apply), the shadow branch is moved to the new commit.
   481→func TestShadow_ShadowBranchMigrationOnPull(t *testing.T) {
   482→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   483→	defer env.Cleanup()
   484→
   485→	env.InitRepo()
   486→
   487→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
   488→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   489→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   490→
   491→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   492→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   493→
   494→	originalHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   495→	originalShadowBranch := env.GetShadowBranchNameForCommit(originalHead)
   496→
   497→	// Start session and create checkpoint
   498→	session := env.NewSession()
   499→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   500→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   501→	}
   502→
   503→	env.WriteFile("file.txt", "content")
   504→	session.CreateTranscript("Add file", []FileChange{{Path: "file.txt", Content: "content"}})
   505→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   506→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Verify shadow branch exists at original commit
   510→	if !env.BranchExists(originalShadowBranch) {
   511→		t.Fatalf("Shadow branch %s should exist", originalShadowBranch)
   512→	}
   513→	t.Logf("Original shadow branch: %s", originalShadowBranch)
   514→
   515→	// Simulate pull: create a new commit (simulating what pull would do)
   516→	// In real scenario: stash → pull → apply
   517→	// Here we just create a commit to simulate HEAD moving
   518→	env.WriteFile("pulled.txt", "from remote")
   519→	env.GitAdd("pulled.txt")
   520→	env.GitCommit("Simulated pull commit")
   521→
   522→	newHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   523→	newShadowBranch := env.GetShadowBranchNameForCommit(newHead)
   524→	t.Logf("After simulated pull: old=%s new=%s", originalHead[:7], newHead[:7])
   525→
   526→	// Restore the file (simulating stash apply)
   527→	env.WriteFile("file.txt", "content")
   528→
   529→	// Next prompt should migrate the shadow branch
   530→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   531→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit after pull failed: %v", err)
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	// Verify old shadow branch is gone and new one exists
   535→	if env.BranchExists(originalShadowBranch) {
   536→		t.Errorf("Old shadow branch %s should be deleted after migration", originalShadowBranch)
   537→	}
   538→	if !env.BranchExists(newShadowBranch) {
   539→		t.Errorf("New shadow branch %s should exist after migration", newShadowBranch)
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Verify we can still create checkpoints on the new shadow branch
   543→	env.WriteFile("file2.txt", "more content")
   544→	session.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   545→	session.CreateTranscript("Add file2", []FileChange{{Path: "file2.txt", Content: "more content"}})
   546→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   547→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop after migration failed: %v", err)
   548→	}
   549→
   550→	// Verify session state has updated base commit and preserves agent type
   551→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(session.ID)
   552→	if err != nil {
   553→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   554→	}
   555→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   556→		t.Errorf("Session base commit should be %s, got %s", newHead[:7], state.BaseCommit[:7])
   557→	}
   558→	if state.StepCount != 2 {
   559→		t.Errorf("Expected 2 checkpoints after migration, got %d", state.StepCount)
   560→	}
   561→	// Verify agent_type is preserved across checkpoints and migration
   562→	expectedAgentType := agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode
   563→	if state.AgentType != expectedAgentType {
   564→		t.Errorf("Session AgentType should be %q, got %q", expectedAgentType, state.AgentType)
   565→	}
   566→
   567→	t.Log("Shadow branch successfully migrated after base commit change")
   568→}
   569→
   570→// TestShadow_ShadowBranchNaming verifies shadow branches follow the
   571→// entire/<base-sha[:7]> naming convention.
   572→func TestShadow_ShadowBranchNaming(t *testing.T) {
   573→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   574→	defer env.Cleanup()
   575→
   576→	env.InitRepo()
   577→
   578→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
   579→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   580→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   581→
   582→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   583→
   584→	// Initialize AFTER branch switch
   585→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   586→
   587→	baseHead := env.GetHeadHash()
   588→
   589→	session := env.NewSession()
   590→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   591→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	env.WriteFile("file.txt", "content")
   595→	session.CreateTranscript("Add file", []FileChange{{Path: "file.txt", Content: "content"}})
   596→
   597→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   598→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	// Verify shadow branch name matches worktree-specific format
   602→	expectedBranch := env.GetShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseHead)
   603→	if !env.BranchExists(expectedBranch) {
   604→		t.Errorf("Shadow branch should be named %s", expectedBranch)
   605→	}
   606→
   607→	// List all entire/ branches
   608→	branches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   609→	t.Logf("Found entire/ branches: %v", branches)
   610→
   611→	foundExpected := false
   612→	for _, b := range branches {
   613→		if b == expectedBranch {
   614→			foundExpected = true
   615→			break
   616→		}
   617→	}
   618→	if !foundExpected {
   619→		t.Errorf("Expected branch %s not found in %v", expectedBranch, branches)
   620→	}
   621→}
   622→
   623→// TestShadow_TranscriptCondensation verifies that session transcripts are
   624→// included in the entire/sessions branch during condensation.
   625→func TestShadow_TranscriptCondensation(t *testing.T) {
   626→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   627→	defer env.Cleanup()
   628→
   629→	// Setup
   630→	env.InitRepo()
   631→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   632→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   633→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   634→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/test")
   635→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   636→
   637→	// Start session and create checkpoint with transcript
   638→	session := env.NewSession()
   639→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   640→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   641→	}
   642→
   643→	// Create a file change
   644→	content := "package main\n\nfunc main() {\n\tprintln(\"hello\")\n}\n"
   645→	env.WriteFile("main.go", content)
   646→
   647→	// Create transcript with meaningful content
   648→	session.CreateTranscript(
   649→		"Create main.go with hello world",
   650→		[]FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: content}},
   651→	)
   652→
   653→	// Save checkpoint (this stores transcript in shadow branch)
   654→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   655→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// Commit with hooks (triggers condensation)
   659→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add main.go", "main.go")
   660→
   661→	// Get checkpoint ID from entire/sessions branch (not from commit message)
   662→	checkpointID := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   663→	t.Logf("Checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   664→
   665→	// Verify entire/sessions branch exists
   666→	if !env.BranchExists(paths.MetadataBranchName) {
   667→		t.Fatal("entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   668→	}
   669→
   670→	// Verify root metadata.json (CheckpointSummary) exists
   671→	summaryPath := CheckpointSummaryPath(checkpointID)
   672→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath) {
   673→		t.Errorf("root metadata.json should exist at %s", summaryPath)
   674→	}
   675→
   676→	// Verify transcript file exists in session subdirectory (new format: 0/full.jsonl)
   677→	transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   678→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath) {
   679→		t.Errorf("Transcript (%s) should exist at %s", paths.TranscriptFileName, transcriptPath)
   680→	} else {
   681→		t.Log("✓ Transcript file exists in checkpoint")
   682→	}
   683→
   684→	// Verify content_hash.txt exists in session subdirectory
   685→	hashPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, "content_hash.txt")
   686→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, hashPath) {
   687→		t.Errorf("content_hash.txt should exist at %s", hashPath)
   688→	}
   689→
   690→	// Verify root metadata.json can be read and parsed as CheckpointSummary
   691→	summaryContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath)
   692→	if !found {
   693→		t.Fatal("root metadata.json should be readable")
   694→	}
   695→	var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
   696→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary); err != nil {
   697→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse root metadata.json as CheckpointSummary: %v", err)
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	// Verify Sessions array is populated
   701→	if len(summary.Sessions) == 0 {
   702→		t.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.Sessions should have at least one entry")
   703→	} else {
   704→		t.Logf("✓ CheckpointSummary has %d session(s)", len(summary.Sessions))
   705→	}
   706→
   707→	// Verify agent field is in session-level metadata (not root summary)
   708→	sessionMetadataPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.MetadataFileName)
   709→	sessionMetadataContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, sessionMetadataPath)
   710→	if !found {
   711→		t.Fatal("session metadata.json should be readable")
   712→	}
   713→	var sessionMetadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   714→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionMetadataContent), &sessionMetadata); err != nil {
   715→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse session metadata.json: %v", err)
   716→	}
   717→	expectedAgent := agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode
   718→	if sessionMetadata.Agent != expectedAgent {
   719→		t.Errorf("session metadata.Agent = %q, want %q", sessionMetadata.Agent, expectedAgent)
   720→	} else {
   721→		t.Logf("✓ Session metadata has agent: %q", sessionMetadata.Agent)
   722→	}
   723→}
   724→
   725→// TestShadow_FullTranscriptContext verifies that each checkpoint includes
   726→// the full session transcript, preserving complete history across commits.
   727→//
   728→// This tests transcript preservation:
   729→// - First commit: context.md includes prompts 1-2
   730→// - Second commit: context.md includes prompts 1-3 (full transcript preserved)
   731→func TestShadow_FullTranscriptContext(t *testing.T) {
   732→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   733→	defer env.Cleanup()
   734→
   735→	// Setup repository
   736→	env.InitRepo()
   737→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   738→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   739→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   740→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/incremental")
   741→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   742→
   743→	t.Log("Phase 1: First session with two prompts")
   744→
   745→	// Start first session
   746→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   747→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   748→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   749→	}
   750→
   751→	// First prompt: create file A
   752→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
   753→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
   754→
   755→	// Build transcript with first prompt
   756→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A in a.go")
   757→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
   758→	toolID1 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
   759→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
   760→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function A!")
   761→
   762→	// Second prompt in same session: create file B
   763→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
   764→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
   765→
   766→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Now create function B in b.go")
   767→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function B for you.")
   768→	toolID2 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
   769→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
   770→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function B!")
   771→
   772→	// Write transcript
   773→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   774→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   775→	}
   776→
   777→	// Save checkpoint (triggers SaveChanges)
   778→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   779→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	t.Log("Phase 2: First user commit")
   783→
   784→	// User commits
   785→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add functions A and B", "a.go", "b.go")
   786→
   787→	// Get first checkpoint ID from commit message trailer
   788→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   789→	checkpoint1ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
   790→	t.Logf("First checkpoint ID: %s", checkpoint1ID)
   791→
   792→	// Verify first checkpoint has both prompts (uses session file path in numbered subdirectory)
   793→	promptPath1 := SessionFilePath(checkpoint1ID, "prompt.txt")
   794→	prompt1Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, promptPath1)
   795→	if !found {
   796→		t.Errorf("prompt.txt should exist at %s", promptPath1)
   797→	} else {
   798→		t.Logf("First prompt.txt content:\n%s", prompt1Content)
   799→		// Should contain both "Create function A" and "create function B"
   800→		if !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "Create function A") {
   801→			t.Error("First prompt.txt should contain 'Create function A'")
   802→		}
   803→		if !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "create function B") {
   804→			t.Error("First prompt.txt should contain 'create function B'")
   805→		}
   806→	}
   807→
   808→	contextPath1 := SessionFilePath(checkpoint1ID, "context.md")
   809→	context1Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, contextPath1)
   810→	if !found {
   811→		t.Errorf("context.md should exist at %s", contextPath1)
   812→	} else {
   813→		t.Logf("First context.md content:\n%s", context1Content)
   814→	}
   815→
   816→	t.Log("Phase 3: Continue session with third prompt")
   817→
   818→	// Continue the session with a new prompt
   819→	// First, simulate another user prompt submit to track the new base
   820→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
   821→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (continued) failed: %v", err)
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	// Third prompt: create file C
   825→	fileCContent := "package main\n\nfunc C() {}\n"
   826→	env.WriteFile("c.go", fileCContent)
   827→
   828→	// Add to transcript (continuing from previous)
   829→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Finally, create function C in c.go")
   830→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function C for you.")
   831→	toolID3 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "c.go", fileCContent)
   832→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID3)
   833→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function C!")
   834→
   835→	// Write updated transcript
   836→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   837→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write updated transcript: %v", err)
   838→	}
   839→
   840→	// Save checkpoint
   841→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   842→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (second) failed: %v", err)
   843→	}
   844→
   845→	t.Log("Phase 4: Second user commit")
   846→
   847→	// User commits again
   848→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function C", "c.go")
   849→
   850→	// Get second checkpoint ID from commit message trailer
   851→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   852→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
   853→	t.Logf("Second checkpoint ID: %s", checkpoint2ID)
   854→
   855→	// Verify different checkpoint IDs
   856→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint2ID {
   857→		t.Errorf("Second commit should have different checkpoint ID: %s vs %s", checkpoint1ID, checkpoint2ID)
   858→	}
   859→
   860→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verify full transcript preserved in second checkpoint")
   861→
   862→	// Verify second checkpoint has the FULL transcript (all three prompts)
   863→	// Session files are now in numbered subdirectories (e.g., 0/prompt.txt)
   864→	promptPath2 := SessionFilePath(checkpoint2ID, "prompt.txt")
   865→	prompt2Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, promptPath2)
   866→	if !found {
   867→		t.Errorf("prompt.txt should exist at %s", promptPath2)
   868→	} else {
   869→		t.Logf("Second prompt.txt content:\n%s", prompt2Content)
   870→
   871→		// Should contain all prompts (full transcript preserved)
   872→		if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "create function C") {
   873→			t.Error("Second prompt.txt should contain 'create function C'")
   874→		}
   875→		if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "Create function A") {
   876→			t.Error("Second prompt.txt should contain 'Create function A' (full transcript)")
   877→		}
   878→		if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "create function B") {
   879→			t.Error("Second prompt.txt should contain 'create function B' (full transcript)")
   880→		}
   881→	}
   882→
   883→	contextPath2 := SessionFilePath(checkpoint2ID, "context.md")
   884→	context2Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, contextPath2)
   885→	if !found {
   886→		t.Errorf("context.md should exist at %s", contextPath2)
   887→	} else {
   888→		t.Logf("Second context.md content:\n%s", context2Content)
   889→
   890→		// Should contain full transcript context
   891→		if !strings.Contains(context2Content, "Create function A") {
   892→			t.Error("Second context.md should contain 'Create function A' (full transcript)")
   893→		}
   894→	}
   895→
   896→	t.Log("Shadow full transcript context test completed successfully!")
   897→}
   898→
   899→// TestShadow_RewindAndCondensation verifies that after rewinding to an earlier
   900→// checkpoint, the checkpoint only includes prompts up to that point.
   901→//
   902→// Workflow:
   903→// 1. Create checkpoint 1 (prompt 1)
   904→// 2. Create checkpoint 2 (prompt 2)
   905→// 3. Rewind to checkpoint 1
   906→// 4. User commits
   907→// 5. Verify checkpoint only contains prompt 1 (NOT prompt 2)
   908→func TestShadow_RewindAndCondensation(t *testing.T) {
   909→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   910→	defer env.Cleanup()
   911→
   912→	// Setup repository
   913→	env.InitRepo()
   914→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
   915→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
   916→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   917→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/rewind-test")
   918→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   919→
   920→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create first checkpoint with prompt 1")
   921→
   922→	session := env.NewSession()
   923→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   924→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   925→	}
   926→
   927→	// First prompt: create file A
   928→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
   929→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
   930→
   931→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A in a.go")
   932→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
   933→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
   934→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
   935→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function A!")
   936→
   937→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   938→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   939→	}
   940→
   941→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   942→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
   943→	}
   944→
   945→	// Get checkpoint 1 for later
   946→	rewindPoints := env.GetRewindPoints()
   947→	if len(rewindPoints) != 1 {
   948→		t.Fatalf("Expected 1 rewind point after checkpoint 1, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
   949→	}
   950→	checkpoint1 := rewindPoints[0]
   951→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1: %s - %s", checkpoint1.ID[:7], checkpoint1.Message)
   952→
   953→	t.Log("Phase 2: Create second checkpoint with prompt 2")
   954→
   955→	// Second prompt: modify file A (a different approach)
   956→	fileAModified := "package main\n\nfunc A() {\n\t// Modified version\n}\n"
   957→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAModified)
   958→
   959→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Actually, modify function A to have a comment")
   960→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll modify function A for you.")
   961→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAModified)
   962→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
   963→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done modifying function A!")
   964→
   965→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   966→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   967→	}
   968→
   969→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   970→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
   971→	}
   972→
   973→	rewindPoints = env.GetRewindPoints()
   974→	if len(rewindPoints) != 2 {
   975→		t.Fatalf("Expected 2 rewind points after checkpoint 2, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
   976→	}
   977→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2: %s - %s", rewindPoints[0].ID[:7], rewindPoints[0].Message)
   978→
   979→	// Verify file has modified content
   980→	currentContent := env.ReadFile("a.go")
   981→	if currentContent != fileAModified {
   982→		t.Errorf("a.go should have modified content before rewind")
   983→	}
   984→
   985→	t.Log("Phase 3: Rewind to checkpoint 1")
   986→
   987→	// Rewind using the CLI (which calls the strategy internally)
   988→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1.ID); err != nil {
   989→		t.Fatalf("Rewind failed: %v", err)
   990→	}
   991→
   992→	// Verify file content is restored to checkpoint 1
   993→	restoredContent := env.ReadFile("a.go")
   994→	if restoredContent != fileAContent {
   995→		t.Errorf("a.go should have original content after rewind.\nExpected:\n%s\nGot:\n%s", fileAContent, restoredContent)
   996→	}
   997→	t.Log("Files successfully restored to checkpoint 1")
   998→
   999→	t.Log("Phase 4: User commits after rewind")
  1000→
  1001→	// User commits - this should trigger condensation
  1002→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function A (reverted)", "a.go")
  1003→
  1004→	// Get checkpoint ID from commit message trailer
  1005→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1006→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
  1007→	t.Logf("Checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
  1008→
  1009→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verify checkpoint only contains prompt 1")
  1010→
  1011→	// Check prompt.txt (uses session file path in numbered subdirectory)
  1012→	promptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, "prompt.txt")
  1013→	promptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, promptPath)
  1014→	if !found {
  1015→		t.Errorf("prompt.txt should exist at %s", promptPath)
  1016→	} else {
  1017→		t.Logf("prompt.txt content:\n%s", promptContent)
  1018→
  1019→		// Should contain prompt 1
  1020→		if !strings.Contains(promptContent, "Create function A") {
  1021→			t.Error("prompt.txt should contain 'Create function A' from checkpoint 1")
  1022→		}
  1023→
  1024→		// Should NOT contain prompt 2 (because we rewound past it)
  1025→		if strings.Contains(promptContent, "modify function A") {
  1026→			t.Error("prompt.txt should NOT contain 'modify function A' - we rewound past that checkpoint")
  1027→		}
  1028→	}
  1029→
  1030→	// Check context.md
  1031→	contextPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, "context.md")
  1032→	contextContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, contextPath)
  1033→	if !found {
  1034→		t.Errorf("context.md should exist at %s", contextPath)
  1035→	} else {
  1036→		t.Logf("context.md content:\n%s", contextContent)
  1037→
  1038→		// Should NOT contain context from checkpoint 2
  1039→		if strings.Contains(contextContent, "modify function A") {
  1040→			t.Error("context.md should NOT contain 'modify function A' - we rewound past that checkpoint")
  1041→		}
  1042→	}
  1043→
  1044→	t.Log("Shadow rewind and condensation test completed successfully!")
  1045→}
  1046→
  1047→// TestShadow_RewindPreservesUntrackedFilesFromSessionStart tests that files that existed
  1048→// in the working directory (but weren't tracked in git) before the session started are
  1049→// preserved when rewinding. This was a bug where such files were incorrectly deleted.
  1050→func TestShadow_RewindPreservesUntrackedFilesFromSessionStart(t *testing.T) {
  1051→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1052→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1053→
  1054→	// Setup repository with initial commit
  1055→	env.InitRepo()
  1056→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1057→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1058→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1059→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/untracked-test")
  1060→
  1061→	// Create an untracked file BEFORE initializing Entire session
  1062→	// This simulates files like .claude/settings.json created by "entire setup"
  1063→	untrackedContent := `{"key": "value"}`
  1064→	env.WriteFile(".claude/settings.json", untrackedContent)
  1065→
  1066→	// Initialize Entire with manual-commit strategy
  1067→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1068→
  1069→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create first checkpoint")
  1070→
  1071→	session := env.NewSession()
  1072→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1073→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1074→	}
  1075→
  1076→	// First prompt: create file A
  1077→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
  1078→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
  1079→
  1080→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A")
  1081→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1082→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
  1083→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
  1084→
  1085→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1086→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1087→	}
  1088→
  1089→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1090→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
  1091→	}
  1092→
  1093→	rewindPoints := env.GetRewindPoints()
  1094→	if len(rewindPoints) != 1 {
  1095→		t.Fatalf("Expected 1 rewind point, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
  1096→	}
  1097→	checkpoint1 := rewindPoints[0]
  1098→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1: %s", checkpoint1.ID[:7])
  1099→
  1100→	t.Log("Phase 2: Create second checkpoint")
  1101→
  1102→	// Second prompt: create file B
  1103→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
  1104→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
  1105→
  1106→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B")
  1107→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1108→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
  1109→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1110→
  1111→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1112→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1113→	}
  1114→
  1115→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1116→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
  1117→	}
  1118→
  1119→	rewindPoints = env.GetRewindPoints()
  1120→	if len(rewindPoints) != 2 {
  1121→		t.Fatalf("Expected 2 rewind points, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
  1122→	}
  1123→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2: %s", rewindPoints[0].ID[:7])
  1124→
  1125→	// Verify the untracked file still exists before rewind
  1126→	if !env.FileExists(".claude/settings.json") {
  1127→		t.Fatal("Untracked file .claude/settings.json should exist before rewind")
  1128→	}
  1129→
  1130→	t.Log("Phase 3: Rewind to checkpoint 1")
  1131→
  1132→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1.ID); err != nil {
  1133→		t.Fatalf("Rewind failed: %v", err)
  1134→	}
  1135→
  1136→	// Verify that the untracked file that existed before session start is PRESERVED
  1137→	if !env.FileExists(".claude/settings.json") {
  1138→		t.Error("CRITICAL: .claude/settings.json was deleted during rewind but it existed before the session started!")
  1139→	} else {
  1140→		restoredContent := env.ReadFile(".claude/settings.json")
  1141→		if restoredContent != untrackedContent {
  1142→			t.Errorf("Untracked file content changed.\nExpected:\n%s\nGot:\n%s", untrackedContent, restoredContent)
  1143→		} else {
  1144→			t.Log("✓ Untracked file .claude/settings.json was preserved correctly")
  1145→		}
  1146→	}
  1147→
  1148→	// Verify b.go was deleted (it was created after checkpoint 1)
  1149→	if env.FileExists("b.go") {
  1150→		t.Error("b.go should have been deleted during rewind (it was created after checkpoint 1)")
  1151→	} else {
  1152→		t.Log("✓ b.go was correctly deleted during rewind")
  1153→	}
  1154→
  1155→	// Verify a.go was restored
  1156→	if !env.FileExists("a.go") {
  1157→		t.Error("a.go should exist after rewind to checkpoint 1")
  1158→	} else {
  1159→		restoredA := env.ReadFile("a.go")
  1160→		if restoredA != fileAContent {
  1161→			t.Errorf("a.go content incorrect after rewind")
  1162→		} else {
  1163→			t.Log("✓ a.go was correctly restored")
  1164→		}
  1165→	}
  1166→
  1167→	t.Log("Test completed successfully!")
  1168→}
  1169→
  1170→// TestShadow_IntermediateCommitsWithoutPrompts tests that commits without new Claude
  1171→// content do NOT get checkpoint trailers.
  1172→//
  1173→// Scenario:
  1174→// 1. Session starts, work happens, checkpoint created
  1175→// 2. First commit gets a trailer (has new content)
  1176→// 3. User commits unrelated files without new Claude work - NO trailer (no new content)
  1177→// 4. User enters new prompt, creates more files
  1178→// 5. Second commit with Claude content gets a trailer
  1179→func TestShadow_IntermediateCommitsWithoutPrompts(t *testing.T) {
  1180→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1181→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1182→
  1183→	// Setup repository
  1184→	env.InitRepo()
  1185→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1186→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1187→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1188→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/intermediate-commits")
  1189→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1190→
  1191→	t.Log("Phase 1: Start session and create checkpoint")
  1192→
  1193→	session := env.NewSession()
  1194→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1195→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1196→	}
  1197→
  1198→	// First prompt: create file A
  1199→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
  1200→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
  1201→
  1202→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A in a.go")
  1203→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
  1204→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
  1205→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
  1206→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function A!")
  1207→
  1208→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1209→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1210→	}
  1211→
  1212→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1213→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1214→	}
  1215→
  1216→	t.Log("Phase 2: First commit (with session content)")
  1217→
  1218→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function A", "a.go")
  1219→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1220→	checkpoint1ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
  1221→	t.Logf("First commit: %s, checkpoint from trailer: %s", commit1Hash[:7], checkpoint1ID)
  1222→	t.Logf("First commit message:\n%s", env.GetCommitMessage(commit1Hash))
  1223→
  1224→	if checkpoint1ID == "" {
  1225→		t.Fatal("First commit should have a checkpoint ID in its trailer (has new content)")
  1226→	}
  1227→
  1228→	t.Log("Phase 3: Create unrelated file and commit WITHOUT new prompt")
  1229→
  1230→	// User creates an unrelated file and commits without entering a new Claude prompt
  1231→	// Since there's no new session content, this commit should NOT get a trailer
  1232→	env.WriteFile("unrelated.txt", "This is an unrelated file")
  1233→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add unrelated file", "unrelated.txt")
  1234→
  1235→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1236→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
  1237→	t.Logf("Second commit: %s, checkpoint from trailer: %s", commit2Hash[:7], checkpoint2ID)
  1238→	t.Logf("Second commit message:\n%s", env.GetCommitMessage(commit2Hash))
  1239→
  1240→	// Second commit should NOT get a checkpoint ID (no new session content)
  1241→	if checkpoint2ID != "" {
  1242→		t.Errorf("Second commit should NOT have a checkpoint trailer (no new content), got: %s", checkpoint2ID)
  1243→	}
  1244→
  1245→	t.Log("Phase 4: New Claude work and commit")
  1246→
  1247→	// Now user enters new prompt and does more work
  1248→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1249→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1250→	}
  1251→
  1252→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
  1253→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
  1254→
  1255→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B in b.go")
  1256→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function B for you.")
  1257→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
  1258→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1259→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function B!")
  1260→
  1261→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1262→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1263→	}
  1264→
  1265→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1266→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1267→	}
  1268→
  1269→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function B", "b.go")
  1270→
  1271→	commit3Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1272→	checkpoint3ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit3Hash)
  1273→	t.Logf("Third commit: %s, checkpoint from trailer: %s", commit3Hash[:7], checkpoint3ID)
  1274→	t.Logf("Third commit message:\n%s", env.GetCommitMessage(commit3Hash))
  1275→
  1276→	if checkpoint3ID == "" {
  1277→		t.Fatal("Third commit should have a checkpoint ID (has new content)")
  1278→	}
  1279→
  1280→	// First and third checkpoint IDs should be different
  1281→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint3ID {
  1282→		t.Errorf("First and third commits should have different checkpoint IDs: %s vs %s",
  1283→			checkpoint1ID, checkpoint3ID)
  1284→	}
  1285→
  1286→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verify checkpoints exist in entire/sessions")
  1287→
  1288→	for _, cpID := range []string{checkpoint1ID, checkpoint3ID} {
  1289→		shardedPath := ShardedCheckpointPath(cpID)
  1290→		metadataPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1291→		if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, metadataPath) {
  1292→			t.Errorf("Checkpoint %s should have metadata.json at %s", cpID, metadataPath)
  1293→		}
  1294→	}
  1295→
  1296→	t.Log("Intermediate commits test completed successfully!")
  1297→}
  1298→
  1299→// TestShadow_FullTranscriptCondensationWithIntermediateCommits tests that full transcripts
  1300→// are preserved across multiple commits.
  1301→//
  1302→// Scenario:
  1303→// 1. Session with prompts A and B, commit 1
  1304→// 2. Continue session with prompt C, commit 2 (without intermediate prompt submit)
  1305→// 3. Verify commit 2's checkpoint has full transcript (A, B, and C)
  1306→func TestShadow_FullTranscriptCondensationWithIntermediateCommits(t *testing.T) {
  1307→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1308→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1309→
  1310→	// Setup repository
  1311→	env.InitRepo()
  1312→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1313→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1314→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1315→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/incremental-intermediate")
  1316→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1317→
  1318→	t.Log("Phase 1: Session with two prompts")
  1319→
  1320→	session := env.NewSession()
  1321→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1322→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1323→	}
  1324→
  1325→	// First prompt
  1326→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
  1327→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
  1328→
  1329→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A")
  1330→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1331→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
  1332→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
  1333→
  1334→	// Second prompt in same session
  1335→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
  1336→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
  1337→
  1338→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B")
  1339→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1340→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
  1341→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1342→
  1343→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1344→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1345→	}
  1346→
  1347→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1348→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1349→	}
  1350→
  1351→	t.Log("Phase 2: First commit")
  1352→
  1353→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add functions A and B", "a.go", "b.go")
  1354→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1355→	checkpoint1ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
  1356→	t.Logf("First commit: %s, checkpoint: %s", commit1Hash[:7], checkpoint1ID)
  1357→
  1358→	// Verify first checkpoint has prompts A and B (session files in numbered subdirectory)
  1359→	prompt1Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, SessionFilePath(checkpoint1ID, "prompt.txt"))
  1360→	if !found {
  1361→		t.Fatal("First checkpoint should have prompt.txt")
  1362→	}
  1363→	if !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "function A") || !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "function B") {
  1364→		t.Errorf("First checkpoint should contain prompts A and B, got: %s", prompt1Content)
  1365→	}
  1366→	t.Logf("First checkpoint prompts:\n%s", prompt1Content)
  1367→
  1368→	t.Log("Phase 3: Continue session with third prompt (no SimulateUserPromptSubmit)")
  1369→
  1370→	// Continue working WITHOUT calling SimulateUserPromptSubmit
  1371→	// This simulates the case where HEAD moved but InitializeSession wasn't called
  1372→	fileCContent := "package main\n\nfunc C() {}\n"
  1373→	env.WriteFile("c.go", fileCContent)
  1374→
  1375→	// Add to transcript
  1376→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function C")
  1377→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1378→	toolID3 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "c.go", fileCContent)
  1379→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID3)
  1380→
  1381→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1382→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write updated transcript: %v", err)
  1383→	}
  1384→
  1385→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1386→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (second) failed: %v", err)
  1387→	}
  1388→
  1389→	t.Log("Phase 4: Second commit (without intermediate prompt submit)")
  1390→
  1391→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add function C", "c.go")
  1392→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1393→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
  1394→	t.Logf("Second commit: %s, checkpoint: %s", commit2Hash[:7], checkpoint2ID)
  1395→
  1396→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint2ID {
  1397→		t.Errorf("Commits should have different checkpoint IDs")
  1398→	}
  1399→
  1400→	t.Log("Phase 5: Verify second checkpoint has full transcript (A, B, and C)")
  1401→
  1402→	// Session files are now in numbered subdirectory (e.g., 0/prompt.txt)
  1403→	prompt2Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, SessionFilePath(checkpoint2ID, "prompt.txt"))
  1404→	if !found {
  1405→		t.Fatal("Second checkpoint should have prompt.txt")
  1406→	}
  1407→
  1408→	t.Logf("Second checkpoint prompts:\n%s", prompt2Content)
  1409→
  1410→	// Should contain all prompts (full transcript preserved)
  1411→	if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "function C") {
  1412→		t.Error("Second checkpoint should contain 'function C'")
  1413→	}
  1414→	if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "function A") {
  1415→		t.Error("Second checkpoint should contain 'function A' (full transcript)")
  1416→	}
  1417→	if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "function B") {
  1418→		t.Error("Second checkpoint should contain 'function B' (full transcript)")
  1419→	}
  1420→
  1421→	t.Log("Full transcript condensation with intermediate commits test completed successfully!")
  1422→}
  1423→
  1424→// TestShadow_RewindPreservesUntrackedFilesWithExistingShadowBranch tests that untracked files
  1425→// present at session start are preserved during rewind, even when the shadow branch already
  1426→// exists from a previous session.
  1427→func TestShadow_RewindPreservesUntrackedFilesWithExistingShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
  1428→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1429→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1430→
  1431→	// Setup repository with initial commit
  1432→	env.InitRepo()
  1433→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1434→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1435→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1436→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/existing-shadow-test")
  1437→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1438→
  1439→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create untracked file before session starts")
  1440→
  1441→	// Create an untracked file BEFORE the first checkpoint
  1442→	// This simulates configuration files that exist before Claude starts
  1443→	untrackedContent := `{"new": "config"}`
  1444→	env.WriteFile(".claude/settings.json", untrackedContent)
  1445→
  1446→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create a previous session to establish shadow branch")
  1447→
  1448→	// First session - creates the shadow branch
  1449→	session1 := env.NewSession()
  1450→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
  1451→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1452→	}
  1453→
  1454→	env.WriteFile("old.go", "package main\n")
  1455→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create old.go")
  1456→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1457→	toolID := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "old.go", "package main\n")
  1458→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID)
  1459→
  1460→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1461→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1462→	}
  1463→
  1464→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1465→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (session 1) failed: %v", err)
  1466→	}
  1467→
  1468→	// Verify shadow branch exists
  1469→	shadowBranchName := env.GetShadowBranchName()
  1470→	if !env.BranchExists(shadowBranchName) {
  1471→		t.Fatalf("Shadow branch %s should exist after first session", shadowBranchName)
  1472→	}
  1473→	t.Logf("Shadow branch %s exists from first session", shadowBranchName)
  1474→
  1475→	t.Log("Phase 2: Continue session and create second checkpoint")
  1476→
  1477→	// Continue the SAME session (Claude resumes with the same session ID)
  1478→	// This is the expected behavior - continuing work on the same base commit
  1479→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
  1480→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (continue session) failed: %v", err)
  1481→	}
  1482→
  1483→	// Reset transcript builder for next checkpoint
  1484→	session1.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
  1485→
  1486→	// Second checkpoint of session - should capture .claude/settings.json
  1487→	env.WriteFile("a.go", "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n")
  1488→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create A")
  1489→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1490→	toolID2 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n")
  1491→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1492→
  1493→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1494→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1495→	}
  1496→
  1497→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1498→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
  1499→	}
  1500→
  1501→	rewindPoints := env.GetRewindPoints()
  1502→	if len(rewindPoints) < 2 {
  1503→		t.Fatalf("Expected at least 2 rewind points, got %d", len(rewindPoints))
  1504→	}
  1505→	// Find the most recent checkpoint (checkpoint 2)
  1506→	checkpoint1 := &rewindPoints[0] // Most recent first
  1507→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 2: %s", checkpoint1.ID[:7])
  1508→
  1509→	t.Log("Phase 3: Create third checkpoint")
  1510→
  1511→	// Continue the session for the third checkpoint
  1512→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID); err != nil {
  1513→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (checkpoint 3) failed: %v", err)
  1514→	}
  1515→
  1516→	// Reset transcript builder for next checkpoint
  1517→	session1.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
  1518→
  1519→	env.WriteFile("b.go", "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n")
  1520→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create B")
  1521→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1522→	toolID3 := session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n")
  1523→	session1.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID3)
  1524→
  1525→	if err := session1.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1526→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1527→	}
  1528→
  1529→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, session1.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1530→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 3) failed: %v", err)
  1531→	}
  1532→
  1533→	t.Log("Phase 4: Rewind to checkpoint 2")
  1534→
  1535→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1.ID); err != nil {
  1536→		t.Fatalf("Rewind failed: %v", err)
  1537→	}
  1538→
  1539→	// Verify that the untracked file that existed at session start is PRESERVED
  1540→	// Since .claude/settings.json was created before checkpoint 1, it's in checkpoint 1's tree
  1541→	// and will flow through to checkpoint 2, so it should be preserved on rewind
  1542→	if !env.FileExists(".claude/settings.json") {
  1543→		t.Error(".claude/settings.json should have been preserved during rewind")
  1544→	} else {
  1545→		restoredContent := env.ReadFile(".claude/settings.json")
  1546→		if restoredContent != untrackedContent {
  1547→			t.Errorf("Untracked file content changed.\nExpected:\n%s\nGot:\n%s", untrackedContent, restoredContent)
  1548→		} else {
  1549→			t.Log("✓ .claude/settings.json was preserved correctly")
  1550→		}
  1551→	}
  1552→
  1553→	// Verify b.go was deleted
  1554→	if env.FileExists("b.go") {
  1555→		t.Error("b.go should have been deleted during rewind")
  1556→	} else {
  1557→		t.Log("✓ b.go was correctly deleted during rewind")
  1558→	}
  1559→
  1560→	t.Log("Test completed successfully!")
  1561→}
  1562→
  1563→// TestShadow_TrailerRemovalSkipsCondensation tests that removing the Entire-Checkpoint
  1564→// trailer during commit message editing causes condensation to be skipped.
  1565→// This allows users to opt-out of linking a commit to their Claude session.
  1566→func TestShadow_TrailerRemovalSkipsCondensation(t *testing.T) {
  1567→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1568→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1569→
  1570→	// Setup
  1571→	env.InitRepo()
  1572→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1573→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1574→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1575→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/trailer-opt-out")
  1576→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1577→
  1578→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create session with content")
  1579→
  1580→	session := env.NewSession()
  1581→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1582→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1583→	}
  1584→
  1585→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc A() {}\n"
  1586→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
  1587→
  1588→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A")
  1589→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
  1590→	toolID := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
  1591→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID)
  1592→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1593→
  1594→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1595→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1596→	}
  1597→
  1598→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1599→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1600→	}
  1601→
  1602→	t.Log("Phase 2: Commit WITH trailer removed (user opts out)")
  1603→
  1604→	// Use the special helper that removes the trailer before committing
  1605→	env.GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved("Add function A (manual commit)", "a.go")
  1606→
  1607→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
  1608→	t.Logf("Commit: %s", commitHash[:7])
  1609→
  1610→	// Verify commit does NOT have trailer
  1611→	commitMsg := env.GetCommitMessage(commitHash)
  1612→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commitMsg); found {
  1613→		t.Errorf("Commit should NOT have Entire-Checkpoint trailer (it was removed), got:\n%s", commitMsg)
  1614→	}
  1615→	t.Logf("Commit message (trailer removed):\n%s", commitMsg)
  1616→
  1617→	t.Log("Phase 3: Verify no condensation happened")
  1618→
  1619→	// entire/sessions branch exists (created at setup), but should not have any checkpoint commits yet
  1620→	// since the user removed the trailer
  1621→	latestCheckpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
  1622→	if latestCheckpointID == "" {
  1623→		t.Log("✓ No checkpoint found on entire/sessions branch (no condensation)")
  1624→	} else {
  1625→		// If there is a checkpoint, this is unexpected for this test
  1626→		t.Logf("Found checkpoint ID: %s (should be from previous activity, not this commit)", latestCheckpointID)
  1627→	}
  1628→
  1629→	t.Log("Phase 4: Now commit WITH trailer (user keeps it)")
  1630→
  1631→	// Continue session with new content
  1632→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1633→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1634→	}
  1635→
  1636→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc B() {}\n"
  1637→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
  1638→
  1639→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B")
  1640→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
  1641→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
  1642→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
  1643→
  1644→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1645→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
  1646→	}
  1647→

  1682→// TestShadow_SessionsBranchCommitTrailers verifies that commits on the entire/sessions
  1683→// branch contain the expected trailers: Entire-Session, Entire-Strategy, and Entire-Agent.
  1684→func TestShadow_SessionsBranchCommitTrailers(t *testing.T) {
  1685→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
  1686→	defer env.Cleanup()
  1687→
  1688→	// Setup
  1689→	env.InitRepo()
  1690→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository")
  1691→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
  1692→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
  1693→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/trailer-test")
  1694→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
  1695→
  1696→	// Start session and create checkpoint
  1697→	session := env.NewSession()
  1698→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
  1699→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
  1700→	}
  1701→
  1702→	fileContent := "package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n"
  1703→	env.WriteFile("main.go", fileContent)
  1704→	session.CreateTranscript("Create main.go", []FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: fileContent}})
  1705→
  1706→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
  1707→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
  1708→	}
  1709→
  1710→	// Commit to trigger condensation
  1711→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add main.go", "main.go")
  1712→
  1713→	// Get the commit message on entire/sessions branch
  1714→	sessionsCommitMsg := env.GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1715→	t.Logf("entire/sessions commit message:\n%s", sessionsCommitMsg)
  1716→
  1717→	// Verify required trailers are present
  1718→	requiredTrailers := map[string]string{
  1719→		trailers.SessionTrailerKey:  "",                                // Entire-Session: <session-id>
  1720→		trailers.StrategyTrailerKey: strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, // Entire-Strategy: manual-commit
  1721→		trailers.AgentTrailerKey:    "Claude Code",                     // Entire-Agent: Claude Code
  1722→	}
  1723→
  1724→	for trailerKey, expectedValue := range requiredTrailers {
  1725→		if !strings.Contains(sessionsCommitMsg, trailerKey+":") {
  1726→			t.Errorf("entire/sessions commit should have %s trailer", trailerKey)
  1727→			continue
  1728→		}
  1729→
  1730→		// If we have an expected value, verify it
  1731→		if expectedValue != "" {
  1732→			expectedTrailer := trailerKey + ": " + expectedValue
  1733→			if !strings.Contains(sessionsCommitMsg, expectedTrailer) {
  1734→				t.Errorf("entire/sessions commit should have %q, got message:\n%s", expectedTrailer, sessionsCommitMsg)
  1735→			} else {
  1736→				t.Logf("✓ Found trailer: %s", expectedTrailer)
  1737→			}
  1738→		} else {
  1739→			t.Logf("✓ Found trailer: %s", trailerKey)
  1740→		}
  1741→	}
  1742→
  1743→	t.Log("Sessions branch commit trailers test completed successfully!")
  1744→}
