    17→// TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop tests the "commit while agent is still working" flow.
    18→//
    19→// When the user commits while the agent is in the ACTIVE phase (between
    20→// SimulateUserPromptSubmit and SimulateStop), the session should transition to
    21→// ACTIVE_COMMITTED. This defers condensation because the agent is still working.
    22→// When the agent finishes its turn (SimulateStop), the deferred condensation fires
    23→// and the session transitions to IDLE with metadata persisted to entire/sessions.
    24→//
    25→// State machine transitions tested:
    26→//   - ACTIVE + GitCommit -> ACTIVE_COMMITTED (defer condensation, migrate shadow branch)
    27→//   - ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd -> IDLE + ActionCondense (deferred condensation fires)
    28→func TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop(t *testing.T) {
    29→	t.Parallel()
    30→
    31→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    32→
    33→	// ========================================
    34→	// Phase 1: Start session and create initial checkpoint
    35→	// ========================================
    36→	t.Log("Phase 1: Start session and create initial work")
    37→
    38→	sess := env.NewSession()
    39→
    40→	// Pass transcript path in the hook input so it's stored in session state
    41→	// (needed for mid-session commit detection via live transcript)
    42→	submitWithTranscriptPath := func(sessionID, transcriptPath string) {
    43→		t.Helper()
    44→		input := map[string]string{
    45→			"session_id":      sessionID,
    46→			"transcript_path": transcriptPath,
    47→		}
    48→		inputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(input)
    49→		cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", "user-prompt-submit")
    50→		cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
    51→		cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
    52→		cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
    53→			"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+env.ClaudeProjectDir,
    54→		)
    55→		if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    56→			t.Fatalf("user-prompt-submit failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
    57→		}
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	submitWithTranscriptPath(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath)
    61→
    62→	// Verify session is ACTIVE
    63→	state, err := env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
    66→	}
    67→	if state == nil {
    68→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist")
    69→	}
    70→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActive {
    71→		t.Errorf("Phase after first prompt should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseActive, state.Phase)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// Create a file and transcript, then stop (first checkpoint)
    75→	env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n\nfunc Feature() {}\n")
    76→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create feature function", []FileChange{
    77→		{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc Feature() {}\n"},
    78→	})
    79→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    80→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Verify session is IDLE after stop
    84→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
    87→	}
    88→	if state == nil {
    89→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist")
    90→	}
    91→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
    92→		t.Errorf("Phase after first stop should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase)
    93→	}
    94→	if state.StepCount != 1 {
    95→		t.Errorf("StepCount after first checkpoint should be 1, got %d", state.StepCount)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Verify shadow branch was created
    99→	initialHead := state.BaseCommit
   100→	shadowBranch := env.GetShadowBranchNameForCommit(initialHead)
   101→	if !env.BranchExists(shadowBranch) {
   102→		t.Fatalf("Shadow branch %s should exist after first checkpoint", shadowBranch)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// ========================================
   106→	// Phase 2: Start new turn and create more work
   107→	// ========================================
   108→	t.Log("Phase 2: Start new turn, create more work")
   109→
   110→	submitWithTranscriptPath(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath)
   111→
   112→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   115→	}
   116→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActive {
   117→		t.Errorf("Phase after second prompt should be %q, got %q", session.PhaseActive, state.Phase)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Create another file and write updated transcript
   121→	env.WriteFile("utils.go", "package main\n\nfunc Util() {}\n")
   122→	sess.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   123→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create utils", []FileChange{
   124→		{Path: "utils.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc Util() {}\n"},
   125→	})
   126→
   127→	// Do NOT stop yet -- the agent is still ACTIVE
   128→
   129→	// ========================================
   130→	// Phase 3: User commits while agent is ACTIVE
   131→	// ========================================
   132→	t.Log("Phase 3: User commits while agent is ACTIVE")
   133→
   134→	headBefore := env.GetHeadHash()
   135→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add feature and utils", "feature.go", "utils.go")
   136→	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   137→
   138→	if commitHash == headBefore {
   139→		t.Fatal("Commit was not created")
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// Verify checkpoint trailer was added
   143→	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
   144→	if checkpointID == "" {
   145→		t.Log("Note: checkpoint trailer may not be present if no shadow branch content was detected (mid-session commit scenario)")
   146→	} else {
   147→		t.Logf("Commit has checkpoint trailer: %s", checkpointID)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	// CRITICAL: Verify session phase is ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   151→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   154→	}
   155→	if state == nil {
   156→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist after commit")
   157→	}
   158→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseActiveCommitted {
   159→		t.Errorf("Phase after commit-while-active should be %q, got %q",
   160→			session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
   161→	}
   162→	t.Logf("Session phase after mid-turn commit: %s", state.Phase)
   163→
   164→	// Verify shadow branch was migrated to the new HEAD
   165→	// The old shadow branch (based on initialHead) may still exist or be cleaned up.
   166→	// The important thing is that the session's BaseCommit was updated.
   167→	if state.BaseCommit == initialHead {
   168→		t.Logf("Note: BaseCommit not yet updated (may happen during migration)")
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// ========================================
   172→	// Phase 4: Agent finishes turn (SimulateStop)
   173→	// ========================================
   174→	t.Log("Phase 4: Agent finishes turn (SimulateStop)")
   175→
   176→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   177→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (after commit) failed: %v", err)
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	// Verify session transitions to IDLE
   181→	state, err = env.GetSessionState(sess.ID)
   182→	if err != nil {
   183→		t.Fatalf("GetSessionState failed: %v", err)
   184→	}
   185→	if state == nil {
   186→		t.Fatal("Session state should exist after stop")
   187→	}
   188→	if state.Phase != session.PhaseIdle {
   189→		t.Errorf("Phase after stop from ACTIVE_COMMITTED should be %q, got %q",
   190→			session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase)
   191→	}
   192→	t.Logf("Session phase after stop: %s (StepCount: %d)", state.Phase, state.StepCount)
   193→
   194→	// Deferred condensation should have fired during TurnEnd (ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE).
   195→	// Verify StepCount was reset and metadata was persisted to entire/sessions.
   196→	if state.StepCount != 0 {
   197→		t.Errorf("StepCount should be 0 after TurnEnd condensation, got %d", state.StepCount)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	if !env.BranchExists(paths.MetadataBranchName) {
   201→		t.Fatal("entire/sessions branch should exist after TurnEnd condensation")
   202→	}
   203→	latestCheckpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
   204→	if latestCheckpointID != "" {
   205→		summaryPath := CheckpointSummaryPath(latestCheckpointID)
   206→		if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath) {
   207→			t.Errorf("Checkpoint metadata should exist at %s", summaryPath)
   208→		} else {
   209→			t.Logf("Condensed data exists at checkpoint %s", latestCheckpointID)
   210→		}
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	t.Log("CommitBeforeStop test completed successfully")
   214→}
   215→
   216→// TestShadow_AmendPreservesTrailer tests that `git commit --amend` preserves
   217→// the checkpoint trailer from the original commit.
   218→//
   219→// When a user amends a commit that has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer, the
   220→// prepare-commit-msg hook (called with source="commit") should preserve the
   221→// existing trailer. No duplicate condensation should occur.
   222→//
   223→// Hook behavior tested:
   224→//   - prepare-commit-msg with source="commit": preserves existing trailer
   225→//   - post-commit after amend: no duplicate condensation
   226→func TestShadow_AmendPreservesTrailer(t *testing.T) {
   227→	t.Parallel()
   228→
   229→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   230→
   231→	// ========================================
   232→	// Phase 1: Full workflow - create checkpoint and commit
   233→	// ========================================
   234→	t.Log("Phase 1: Create session, checkpoint, and commit")
   235→
   236→	sess := env.NewSession()
   237→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(sess.ID); err != nil {
   238→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Create a file and checkpoint
   242→	env.WriteFile("main.go", "package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n")
   243→	sess.CreateTranscript("Create main.go", []FileChange{
   244→		{Path: "main.go", Content: "package main\n\nfunc main() {}\n"},
   245→	})
   246→	if err := env.SimulateStop(sess.ID, sess.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   247→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	// Commit with hooks (triggers condensation)
   251→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Initial implementation", "main.go")
   252→
   253→	originalCommitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   254→	originalCheckpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(originalCommitHash)
   255→	if originalCheckpointID == "" {
   256→		t.Fatal("Original commit should have a checkpoint trailer")
   257→	}
   258→	t.Logf("Original commit %s has checkpoint ID: %s", originalCommitHash[:7], originalCheckpointID)
   259→
   260→	// Verify condensation happened
   261→	if !env.BranchExists(paths.MetadataBranchName) {
   262→		t.Fatal("entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Record the sessions branch state for later comparison
   266→	sessionsCommitBefore := env.GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   267→	t.Logf("Sessions branch commit before amend:\n%s", sessionsCommitBefore)
   268→
   269→	// ========================================
   270→	// Phase 2: Amend the commit
   271→	// ========================================
   272→	t.Log("Phase 2: Amend the commit")
   273→
   274→	// Amend with the same message (simulating a minor message edit or staging additional files)
   275→	env.GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks("Initial implementation (amended)")
   276→
   277→	amendedCommitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   278→	t.Logf("Amended commit: %s", amendedCommitHash[:7])
   279→
   280→	// The amended commit hash should be different from the original
   281→	if amendedCommitHash == originalCommitHash {
   282→		t.Error("Amended commit should have a different hash")
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// ========================================
   286→	// Phase 3: Verify trailer is preserved
   287→	// ========================================
   288→	t.Log("Phase 3: Verify checkpoint trailer is preserved")
   289→
   290→	amendedCheckpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(amendedCommitHash)
   291→	if amendedCheckpointID == "" {
   292→		t.Fatal("Amended commit should still have a checkpoint trailer")
   293→	}
   294→	t.Logf("Amended commit has checkpoint ID: %s", amendedCheckpointID)
   295→
   296→	// The checkpoint ID should be the SAME as the original
   297→	if amendedCheckpointID != originalCheckpointID {
   298→		t.Errorf("Amended commit should preserve original checkpoint ID.\nOriginal: %s\nAmended:  %s",
   299→			originalCheckpointID, amendedCheckpointID)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	// ========================================
   303→	// Phase 4: Verify no duplicate condensation
   304→	// ========================================
   305→	t.Log("Phase 4: Verify no duplicate condensation")
   306→
   307→	// The sessions branch commit should not have changed (no new condensation)
   308→	sessionsCommitAfter := env.GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   309→	if sessionsCommitBefore != sessionsCommitAfter {
   310→		t.Logf("Sessions branch commit changed after amend:\nBefore:\n%s\nAfter:\n%s",
   311→			sessionsCommitBefore, sessionsCommitAfter)
   312→		// This is not necessarily an error -- post-commit might trigger condensation
   313→		// if there's new content. But with no new session activity, it should be the same.
   314→		t.Log("Note: sessions branch was updated (may indicate duplicate condensation)")
   315→	} else {
   316→		t.Log("Sessions branch unchanged after amend (no duplicate condensation)")
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	// Verify the checkpoint data still exists and is accessible
   320→	summaryPath := CheckpointSummaryPath(originalCheckpointID)
   321→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath) {
   322→		t.Errorf("Checkpoint metadata should still exist at %s after amend", summaryPath)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(originalCheckpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   326→	if !env.FileExistsInBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath) {
   327→		t.Errorf("Transcript should still exist at %s after amend", transcriptPath)
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	t.Log("AmendPreservesTrailer test completed successfully")
   331→}

    18→// TestManualCommit_Attribution tests the full attribution calculation flow:
    19→// 1. Agent creates checkpoint 1
    20→// 2. User makes changes between checkpoints
    21→// 3. User enters new prompt (attribution calculated at prompt start)
    22→// 4. Agent creates checkpoint 2
    23→// 5. User commits (condensation happens with attribution)
    24→// 6. Verify attribution metadata is correct
    25→func TestManualCommit_Attribution(t *testing.T) {
    26→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    27→	defer env.Cleanup()
    28→
    29→	env.InitRepo()
    30→
    31→	// Create initial commit
    32→	env.WriteFile("main.go", "package main\n")
    33→	env.GitAdd("main.go")
    34→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    35→
    36→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
    37→
    38→	initialHead := env.GetHeadHash()
    39→	t.Logf("Initial HEAD: %s", initialHead[:7])
    40→
    41→	// ========================================
    42→	// CHECKPOINT 1: Agent adds function
    43→	// ========================================
    44→	t.Log("Creating checkpoint 1 (agent adds function)")
    45→
    46→	session := env.NewSession()
    47→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
    48→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (prompt 1) failed: %v", err)
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	// Agent adds 4 lines
    52→	checkpoint1Content := "package main\n\nfunc agentFunc() {\n\treturn 42\n}\n"
    53→	env.WriteFile("main.go", checkpoint1Content)
    54→
    55→	session.CreateTranscript(
    56→		"Add agent function",
    57→		[]FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: checkpoint1Content}},
    58→	)
    59→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    60→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 1) failed: %v", err)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	// ========================================
    64→	// USER EDITS between checkpoints
    65→	// ========================================
    66→	t.Log("User makes edits between checkpoints")
    67→
    68→	// User adds 5 comment lines
    69→	userContent := checkpoint1Content +
    70→		"// User comment 1\n" +
    71→		"// User comment 2\n" +
    72→		"// User comment 3\n" +
    73→		"// User comment 4\n" +
    74→		"// User comment 5\n"
    75→	env.WriteFile("main.go", userContent)
    76→
    77→	// ========================================
    78→	// CHECKPOINT 2: New prompt (attribution calculated)
    79→	// ========================================
    80→	t.Log("User enters new prompt (attribution should capture 5 user lines)")
    81→
    82→	// Simulate UserPromptSubmit hook - this calculates attribution at prompt start
    83→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
    84→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (prompt 2) failed: %v", err)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Agent adds another function (4 more lines)
    88→	checkpoint2Content := userContent + "\nfunc agentFunc2() {\n\treturn 100\n}\n"
    89→	env.WriteFile("main.go", checkpoint2Content)
    90→
    91→	session.CreateTranscript(
    92→		"Add second agent function",
    93→		[]FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: checkpoint2Content}},
    94→	)
    95→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    96→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (checkpoint 2) failed: %v", err)
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Verify 2 rewind points
   100→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   101→	if len(points) != 2 {
   102→		t.Fatalf("Expected 2 rewind points, got %d", len(points))
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// ========================================
   106→	// USER COMMITS: Condensation happens
   107→	// ========================================
   108→	t.Log("User commits (condensation should happen)")
   109→
   110→	// Commit using hooks (this triggers condensation)
   111→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add functions", "main.go")
   112→
   113→	// Get commit hash and checkpoint ID
   114→	headHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   115→	t.Logf("User commit: %s", headHash[:7])
   116→
   117→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   118→	if err != nil {
   119→		t.Fatalf("failed to open repo: %v", err)
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	commitObj, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(headHash))
   123→	if err != nil {
   124→		t.Fatalf("failed to get commit object: %v", err)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commitObj.Message)
   128→	if !found {
   129→		t.Fatal("Commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer")
   130→	}
   131→	t.Logf("Checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   132→
   133→	// ========================================
   134→	// VERIFY ATTRIBUTION
   135→	// ========================================
   136→	t.Log("Verifying attribution in metadata")
   137→
   138→	// Read metadata from entire/sessions branch
   139→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   140→	if err != nil {
   141→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get entire/sessions branch: %v", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	sessionsCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(sessionsRef.Hash())
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get sessions commit: %v", err)
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	sessionsTree, err := sessionsCommit.Tree()
   150→	if err != nil {
   151→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get sessions tree: %v", err)
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	// Read session-level metadata.json from sharded path (InitialAttribution is in 0/metadata.json)
   155→	metadataPath := SessionMetadataPath(checkpointID.String())
   156→	metadataFile, err := sessionsTree.File(metadataPath)
   157→	if err != nil {
   158→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session metadata.json at path %s: %v", metadataPath, err)
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	metadataContent, err := metadataFile.Contents()
   162→	if err != nil {
   163→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read metadata content: %v", err)
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	var metadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   167→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(metadataContent), &metadata); err != nil {
   168→		t.Fatalf("Failed to parse metadata.json: %v", err)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Verify InitialAttribution exists
   172→	if metadata.InitialAttribution == nil {
   173→		t.Fatal("InitialAttribution is nil")
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	attr := metadata.InitialAttribution
   177→	t.Logf("Attribution: agent=%d, human_added=%d, human_modified=%d, human_removed=%d, total=%d, percentage=%.1f%%",
   178→		attr.AgentLines, attr.HumanAdded, attr.HumanModified, attr.HumanRemoved,
   179→		attr.TotalCommitted, attr.AgentPercentage)
   180→
   181→	// Verify attribution was calculated and has reasonable values
   182→	// Note: The shadow branch includes all worktree changes (agent + user),
   183→	// so base→shadow diff includes user edits that were present during SaveChanges.
   184→	// The attribution separates them using PromptAttributions.
   185→	//
   186→	// Expected: agent=13 (base→shadow includes user comments in worktree)
   187→	//           human=5 (from PromptAttribution)
   188→	//           total=18 (net additions)
   189→	//
   190→	// This tests that:
   191→	// 1. Attribution is calculated and stored
   192→	// 2. PromptAttribution captured user edits between checkpoints
   193→	// 3. Percentages are computed
   194→	if attr.AgentLines <= 0 {
   195→		t.Errorf("AgentLines = %d, should be > 0", attr.AgentLines)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	if attr.HumanAdded != 5 {
   199→		t.Errorf("HumanAdded = %d, want 5 (5 comments captured in PromptAttribution)",
   200→			attr.HumanAdded)
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	if attr.TotalCommitted <= 0 {
   204→		t.Errorf("TotalCommitted = %d, should be > 0", attr.TotalCommitted)
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	if attr.AgentPercentage <= 0 || attr.AgentPercentage >= 100 {
   208→		t.Errorf("AgentPercentage = %.1f%%, should be between 0 and 100",
   209→			attr.AgentPercentage)
   210→	}
   211→}

   213→// TestManualCommit_AttributionDeletionOnly tests attribution for deletion-only commits
   214→func TestManualCommit_AttributionDeletionOnly(t *testing.T) {
   215→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   216→	defer env.Cleanup()
   217→
   218→	env.InitRepo()
   219→
   220→	// Create initial commit with content
   221→	initialContent := "package main\n\nfunc oldFunc1() {}\nfunc oldFunc2() {}\nfunc oldFunc3() {}\n"
   222→	env.WriteFile("main.go", initialContent)
   223→	env.GitAdd("main.go")
   224→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   225→
   226→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   227→
   228→	// ========================================
   229→	// CHECKPOINT 1: Agent REMOVES a function (deletion, no additions)
   230→	// ========================================
   231→	session := env.NewSession()
   232→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   233→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	// Agent removes one function (keeps 2 functions)
   237→	checkpointContent := "package main\n\nfunc oldFunc2() {}\nfunc oldFunc3() {}\n"
   238→	env.WriteFile("main.go", checkpointContent)
   239→
   240→	session.CreateTranscript(
   241→		"Remove oldFunc1",
   242→		[]FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: checkpointContent}},
   243→	)
   244→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   245→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	// ========================================
   249→	// USER DELETES REMAINING FUNCTIONS
   250→	// ========================================
   251→	t.Log("User deletes remaining functions (deletion-only commit)")
   252→
   253→	// Remove remaining functions, keep only package declaration
   254→	env.WriteFile("main.go", "package main\n")
   255→
   256→	// Commit using hooks
   257→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Remove remaining functions", "main.go")
   258→
   259→	// Get checkpoint ID
   260→	headHash := env.GetHeadHash()
   261→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   262→	if err != nil {
   263→		t.Fatalf("failed to open repo: %v", err)
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	commitObj, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(headHash))
   267→	if err != nil {
   268→		t.Fatalf("failed to get commit object: %v", err)
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commitObj.Message)
   272→	if !found {
   273→		t.Fatal("Commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer")
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// ========================================
   277→	// VERIFY ATTRIBUTION FOR DELETION-ONLY COMMIT
   278→	// ========================================
   279→	t.Log("Verifying attribution for deletion-only commit")
   280→
   281→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   282→	if err != nil {
   283→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get entire/sessions branch: %v", err)
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	sessionsCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(sessionsRef.Hash())
   287→	if err != nil {
   288→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get sessions commit: %v", err)
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	sessionsTree, err := sessionsCommit.Tree()
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get sessions tree: %v", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Read session-level metadata.json (InitialAttribution is in 0/metadata.json)
   297→	metadataPath := SessionMetadataPath(checkpointID.String())
   298→	metadataFile, err := sessionsTree.File(metadataPath)
   299→	if err != nil {
   300→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session metadata.json at path %s: %v", metadataPath, err)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	metadataContent, err := metadataFile.Contents()
   304→	if err != nil {
   305→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read metadata content: %v", err)
   306→	}
   307→
   308→	var metadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   309→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(metadataContent), &metadata); err != nil {
   310→		t.Fatalf("Failed to parse metadata.json: %v", err)
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	if metadata.InitialAttribution == nil {
   314→		t.Fatal("InitialAttribution is nil")
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	attr := metadata.InitialAttribution
   318→	t.Logf("Attribution (deletion-only): agent=%d, human_added=%d, human_removed=%d, total=%d, percentage=%.1f%%",
   319→		attr.AgentLines, attr.HumanAdded, attr.HumanRemoved,
   320→		attr.TotalCommitted, attr.AgentPercentage)
   321→
   322→	// For deletion-only commits where agent makes no additions:
   323→	// - Agent removed oldFunc1 (made deletions, not additions)
   324→	// - AgentLines = 0 (no additions)
   325→	// - User removed oldFunc2 and oldFunc3
   326→	// - HumanAdded = 0 (no new lines)
   327→	// - HumanRemoved = number of lines user deleted
   328→	// - TotalCommitted = 0 (no additions from anyone)
   329→	// - AgentPercentage = 0 (by convention for deletion-only)
   330→
   331→	if attr.AgentLines != 0 {
   332→		t.Errorf("AgentLines = %d, want 0 (agent made no additions, only deletions)", attr.AgentLines)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	if attr.HumanAdded != 0 {
   336→		t.Errorf("HumanAdded = %d, want 0 (no new lines in deletion-only commit)", attr.HumanAdded)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	// User removed 2 remaining functions + 1 blank line (3 lines total)
   340→	if attr.HumanRemoved != 3 {
   341→		t.Errorf("HumanRemoved = %d, want 3 (removed blank + 2 functions = 3 lines)", attr.HumanRemoved)
   342→	}
   343→
   344→	if attr.TotalCommitted != 0 {
   345→		t.Errorf("TotalCommitted = %d, want 0 (deletion-only commit has no net additions)", attr.TotalCommitted)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	if attr.AgentPercentage != 0 {
   349→		t.Errorf("AgentPercentage = %.1f%%, want 0 (deletion-only commit)",
   350→			attr.AgentPercentage)
   351→	}
   352→}
   353→
   354→// TestManualCommit_AttributionNoDoubleCount tests that PromptAttributions are
   355→// cleared after condensation to prevent double-counting on subsequent commits.
   356→//
   357→// Bug scenario:
   358→// 1. Checkpoint 1 → user edits → commit (condensation, PromptAttributions used)
   359→// 2. StepCount reset to 0, but PromptAttributions NOT cleared
   360→// 3. Checkpoint 2 → new PromptAttributions appended to old ones
   361→// 4. Second commit → CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated sums ALL PromptAttributions
   362→// 5. User edits from first commit are double-counted
   363→func TestManualCommit_AttributionNoDoubleCount(t *testing.T) {
   364→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
   365→	defer env.Cleanup()
   366→
   367→	env.InitRepo()
   368→
   369→	// Create initial commit
   370→	env.WriteFile("main.go", "package main\n")
   371→	env.GitAdd("main.go")
   372→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
   373→
   374→	env.InitEntire(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   375→
   376→	// ========================================
   377→	// FIRST CYCLE: Checkpoint → user edit → commit
   378→	// ========================================
   379→	t.Log("First cycle: agent checkpoint + user edit + commit")
   380→
   381→	session := env.NewSession()
   382→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   383→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (first cycle) failed: %v", err)
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	// Agent adds 5 lines
   387→	checkpoint1Content := "package main\n\nfunc agent1() { return 1 }\nfunc agent2() { return 2 }\nfunc agent3() { return 3 }\n"
   388→	env.WriteFile("main.go", checkpoint1Content)
   389→
   390→	session.CreateTranscript(
   391→		"Add agent functions",
   392→		[]FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: checkpoint1Content}},
   393→	)
   394→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   395→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (first cycle) failed: %v", err)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	// User adds 2 lines between checkpoints
   399→	userEdit1Content := checkpoint1Content + "// User comment 1\n// User comment 2\n"
   400→	env.WriteFile("main.go", userEdit1Content)
   401→
   402→	// Commit with hooks (condensation happens)
   403→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("First commit", "main.go")
   404→
   405→	// Get first commit's checkpoint ID
   406→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   407→	if err != nil {
   408→		t.Fatalf("failed to open repo: %v", err)
   409→	}
   410→
   411→	head, err := repo.Head()
   412→	if err != nil {
   413→		t.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	commit1, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   417→	if err != nil {
   418→		t.Fatalf("failed to get commit: %v", err)
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	checkpointID1, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit1.Message)
   422→	if !found {
   423→		t.Fatal("First commit should have checkpoint trailer")
   424→	}
   425→
   426→	t.Logf("First commit checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID1)
   427→
   428→	// Verify first commit attribution
   429→	attr1 := getAttributionFromMetadata(t, repo, checkpointID1)
   430→	t.Logf("First commit attribution: agent=%d, human_added=%d, total=%d",
   431→		attr1.AgentLines, attr1.HumanAdded, attr1.TotalCommitted)
   432→
   433→	// First commit should have:
   434→	// - Agent: 4 lines (3 functions + 1 blank)
   435→	// - User: 2 lines (2 comments)
   436→	// - Total: 6 lines
   437→	if attr1.HumanAdded != 2 {
   438→		t.Errorf("First commit HumanAdded = %d, want 2", attr1.HumanAdded)
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	// ========================================
   442→	// SECOND CYCLE: New checkpoint → user edit → commit
   443→	// ========================================
   444→	t.Log("Second cycle: new agent checkpoint + user edit + commit")
   445→
   446→	// Simulate new prompt (should calculate attribution, which should be empty after reset)
   447→	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
   448→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit (second cycle) failed: %v", err)
   449→	}
   450→
   451→	// Agent adds 3 more lines
   452→	checkpoint2Content := userEdit1Content + "\nfunc agent4() { return 4 }\nfunc agent5() { return 5 }\n"
   453→	env.WriteFile("main.go", checkpoint2Content)
   454→
   455→	session.CreateTranscript(
   456→		"Add more agent functions",
   457→		[]FileChange{{Path: "main.go", Content: checkpoint2Content}},
   458→	)
   459→	if err := env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   460→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop (second cycle) failed: %v", err)
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// User adds 1 more line
   464→	userEdit2Content := checkpoint2Content + "// User comment 3\n"
   465→	env.WriteFile("main.go", userEdit2Content)
   466→
   467→	// Second commit (another condensation)
   468→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Second commit", "main.go")
   469→
   470→	// Get second commit's checkpoint ID
   471→	head, err = repo.Head()
   472→	if err != nil {
   473→		t.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD after second commit: %v", err)
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	commit2, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   477→	if err != nil {
   478→		t.Fatalf("failed to get second commit: %v", err)
   479→	}
   480→
   481→	checkpointID2, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit2.Message)
   482→	if !found {
   483→		t.Fatal("Second commit should have checkpoint trailer")
   484→	}
   485→
   486→	t.Logf("Second commit checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID2)
   487→
   488→	// Verify second commit attribution
   489→	attr2 := getAttributionFromMetadata(t, repo, checkpointID2)
   490→	t.Logf("Second commit attribution: agent=%d, human_added=%d, total=%d",
   491→		attr2.AgentLines, attr2.HumanAdded, attr2.TotalCommitted)
   492→
   493→	// Second commit should have (since first commit):
   494→	// - Agent: 3 lines (2 functions + 1 blank)
   495→	// - User: 1 line (1 comment)
   496→	// - Total: 4 lines
   497→	//
   498→	// BUG (if not fixed): HumanAdded would be 3 (1 new + 2 from first commit double-counted)
   499→	// CORRECT (after fix): HumanAdded should be 1 (only new user edits)
   500→
   501→	if attr2.HumanAdded != 1 {
   502→		t.Errorf("Second commit HumanAdded = %d, want 1 (should NOT double-count first commit's 2 user lines)",
   503→			attr2.HumanAdded)
   504→	}
   505→
   506→	if attr2.TotalCommitted != 4 {
   507→		t.Errorf("Second commit TotalCommitted = %d, want 4 (3 agent + 1 user)",
   508→			attr2.TotalCommitted)
   509→	}
   510→
   511→	// Agent percentage should be 3/4 = 75%
   512→	if attr2.AgentPercentage < 74.9 || attr2.AgentPercentage > 75.1 {
   513→		t.Errorf("Second commit AgentPercentage = %.1f%%, want 75.0%%", attr2.AgentPercentage)
   514→	}
   515→}
   516→
   517→// getAttributionFromMetadata reads attribution from a checkpoint on entire/sessions branch.
   518→// InitialAttribution is stored in session-level metadata (0/metadata.json).
   519→func getAttributionFromMetadata(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) *checkpoint.InitialAttribution {
   520→	t.Helper()
   521→
   522→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   523→	if err != nil {
   524→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get entire/sessions branch: %v", err)
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	sessionsCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(sessionsRef.Hash())
   528→	if err != nil {
   529→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get sessions commit: %v", err)
   530→	}
   531→
   532→	sessionsTree, err := sessionsCommit.Tree()
   533→	if err != nil {
   534→		t.Fatalf("Failed to get sessions tree: %v", err)
   535→	}
   536→
   537→	// Read session-level metadata (InitialAttribution is in 0/metadata.json)
   538→	metadataPath := SessionMetadataPath(checkpointID.String())
   539→	metadataFile, err := sessionsTree.File(metadataPath)
   540→	if err != nil {
   541→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read session metadata.json at path %s: %v", metadataPath, err)
   542→	}
   543→
   544→	metadataContent, err := metadataFile.Contents()
   545→	if err != nil {
   546→		t.Fatalf("Failed to read metadata content: %v", err)
   547→	}
   548→
   549→	var metadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
   550→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(metadataContent), &metadata); err != nil {
   551→		t.Fatalf("Failed to parse metadata.json: %v", err)
   552→	}
   553→
   554→	if metadata.InitialAttribution == nil {
   555→		t.Fatal("InitialAttribution is nil")
   556→	}
   557→
   558→	return metadata.InitialAttribution
   559→}

    14→// TestDualStrategy_NoCheckpointForNoChanges verifies that the auto-commit strategy
    15→// does NOT create a checkpoint when a prompt results in no file changes,
    16→// even after a previous prompt that DID create file changes.
    17→//
    18→// This is the fix for ENT-70: auto-commit strategy was incorrectly triggering checkpoints
    19→// because it parsed the entire transcript including file changes from previous prompts.
    20→func TestDualStrategy_NoCheckpointForNoChanges(t *testing.T) {
    21→	t.Parallel()
    22→
    23→	// Only run for auto-commit strategy
    24→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit)
    25→
    26→	// Create a session
    27→	session := env.NewSession()
    28→
    29→	// === FIRST PROMPT: Creates a file ===
    30→	err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
    31→	if err != nil {
    32→		t.Fatalf("First SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
    33→	}
    34→
    35→	// Create a file (as if Claude Code wrote it)
    36→	env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package feature\n\nfunc Hello() {}\n")
    37→
    38→	// Create transcript for first prompt
    39→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create a hello function")
    40→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create that for you.")

   121→// TestDualStrategy_IncrementalPromptContent verifies that each checkpoint only
   122→// includes prompts since the last checkpoint, not the entire session history.
   123→//
   124→// This is the auto-commit equivalent of the manual-commit incremental condensation test.
   125→// For auto-commit strategy, each checkpoint creates a commit, so the prompt.txt should only
   126→// contain prompts from that specific checkpoint, not previous ones.
   127→func TestDualStrategy_IncrementalPromptContent(t *testing.T) {
   128→	t.Parallel()
   129→
   130→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit)
   131→	session := env.NewSession()
   132→
   133→	// === FIRST PROMPT: Creates file A ===
   134→	t.Log("Phase 1: First prompt creates file A")
   135→
   136→	err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   137→	if err != nil {
   138→		t.Fatalf("First SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	fileAContent := "package main\n\nfunc FunctionA() {}\n"
   142→	env.WriteFile("a.go", fileAContent)
   143→
   144→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function A for the first feature")
   145→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function A for you.")
   146→	toolID1 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "a.go", fileAContent)
   147→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID1)
   148→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function A!")
   149→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   150→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	err = env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath)
   154→	if err != nil {
   155→		t.Fatalf("First SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Get checkpoint ID from first commit
   159→	commit1Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   160→	checkpoint1ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit1Hash)
   161→	t.Logf("First checkpoint: %s (commit %s)", checkpoint1ID, commit1Hash[:7])
   162→
   163→	// Verify first checkpoint has prompt A (session files in numbered subdirectory)
   164→	prompt1Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, SessionFilePath(checkpoint1ID, "prompt.txt"))
   165→	if !found {
   166→		t.Fatal("First checkpoint should have prompt.txt on entire/sessions branch")
   167→	}
   168→	t.Logf("First checkpoint prompt.txt:\n%s", prompt1Content)
   169→
   170→	if !strings.Contains(prompt1Content, "function A") {
   171→		t.Error("First checkpoint prompt.txt should contain 'function A'")
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	// === SECOND PROMPT: Creates file B ===
   175→	t.Log("Phase 2: Second prompt creates file B")
   176→
   177→	err = env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		t.Fatalf("Second SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	fileBContent := "package main\n\nfunc FunctionB() {}\n"
   183→	env.WriteFile("b.go", fileBContent)
   184→
   185→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Create function B for the second feature")
   186→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll create function B for you.")
   187→	toolID2 := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "b.go", fileBContent)
   188→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID2)
   189→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done creating function B!")
   190→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   191→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	err = env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath)
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		t.Fatalf("Second SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	// Get checkpoint ID from second commit
   200→	commit2Hash := env.GetHeadHash()
   201→	checkpoint2ID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commit2Hash)
   202→	t.Logf("Second checkpoint: %s (commit %s)", checkpoint2ID, commit2Hash[:7])
   203→
   204→	if checkpoint1ID == checkpoint2ID {
   205→		t.Error("Checkpoints should have different IDs")
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// === VERIFY INCREMENTAL CONTENT ===
   209→	t.Log("Phase 3: Verify second checkpoint only has prompt B (incremental)")
   210→
   211→	// Session files are now in numbered subdirectory (e.g., 0/prompt.txt)
   212→	prompt2Content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, SessionFilePath(checkpoint2ID, "prompt.txt"))
   213→	if !found {
   214→		t.Fatal("Second checkpoint should have prompt.txt on entire/sessions branch")
   215→	}
   216→	t.Logf("Second checkpoint prompt.txt:\n%s", prompt2Content)
   217→
   218→	// Should contain prompt B
   219→	if !strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "function B") {
   220→		t.Error("Second checkpoint prompt.txt should contain 'function B'")
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	// Should NOT contain prompt A (already in first checkpoint)
   224→	if strings.Contains(prompt2Content, "function A") {
   225→		t.Error("Second checkpoint prompt.txt should NOT contain 'function A' (already in first checkpoint)")
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	t.Log("Incremental prompt content test completed successfully!")
   229→}

   231→// TestDualStrategy_SessionStateTracksTranscriptOffset verifies that session state
   232→// correctly tracks the transcript offset (CheckpointTranscriptStart) across prompts.
   233→// Note: cannot use t.Parallel() because we need t.Chdir to load session state.
   234→func TestDualStrategy_SessionStateTracksTranscriptOffset(t *testing.T) {
   235→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit)
   236→	session := env.NewSession()
   237→	entireSessionID := sessionid.EntireSessionID(session.ID)
   238→
   239→	// First prompt
   240→	err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   241→	if err != nil {
   242→		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	// Session state is created by InitializeSession during UserPromptSubmit
   246→	// We need to change to the repo directory to load session state (it uses GetGitCommonDir)
   247→	t.Chdir(env.RepoDir)
   248→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID)
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		t.Fatalf("LoadSessionState failed: %v", err)
   251→	}
   252→	if state == nil {
   253→		t.Fatal("Session state should have been created by InitializeSession")
   254→	}
   255→	if state.CheckpointTranscriptStart != 0 {
   256→		t.Errorf("Initial CheckpointTranscriptStart should be 0, got %d", state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   257→	}
   258→	if state.StepCount != 0 {
   259→		t.Errorf("Initial StepCount should be 0, got %d", state.StepCount)
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	// Create file and transcript
   263→	env.WriteFile("test.go", "package test")
   264→	session.CreateTranscript("Create test file", []FileChange{
   265→		{Path: "test.go", Content: "package test"},
   266→	})
   267→
   268→	// Simulate stop - this should update CheckpointTranscriptStart
   269→	err = env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath)
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		t.Fatalf("SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	// Verify session state was updated with transcript position
   275→	state, err = strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID)
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		t.Fatalf("LoadSessionState after stop failed: %v", err)
   278→	}
   279→	if state.CheckpointTranscriptStart == 0 {
   280→		t.Error("CheckpointTranscriptStart should have been updated after checkpoint")
   281→	}
   282→	if state.StepCount != 1 {
   283→		t.Errorf("StepCount should be 1, got %d", state.StepCount)
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	// Second prompt - add more to transcript
   287→	err = env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID)
   288→	if err != nil {
   289→		t.Fatalf("Second SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// Modify a file
   293→	env.WriteFile("test.go", "package test\n\nfunc Test() {}\n")
   294→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage("Add a test function")
   295→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Adding test function.")
   296→	toolID := session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", "test.go", "package test\n\nfunc Test() {}\n")
   297→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID)
   298→	session.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
   299→	if err := session.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   300→		t.Fatalf("Failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	// Simulate second stop
   304→	err = env.SimulateStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath)
   305→	if err != nil {
   306→		t.Fatalf("Second SimulateStop failed: %v", err)
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	// Verify session state was updated again
   310→	state, err = strategy.LoadSessionState(entireSessionID)
   311→	if err != nil {
   312→		t.Fatalf("LoadSessionState after second stop failed: %v", err)
   313→	}
   314→	if state.StepCount != 2 {
   315→		t.Errorf("StepCount should be 2, got %d", state.StepCount)
   316→	}
   317→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart should be higher than after first stop
   318→	t.Logf("Final CheckpointTranscriptStart: %d, StepCount: %d",
   319→		state.CheckpointTranscriptStart, state.StepCount)
   320→}

> AGENT

You have excellent coverage here. Here's a summary of what's tested for the "commits during a session get checkpoint trailers + condensation works" flow:

## Integration Tests - Checkpoint Trailers & Condensation

### Manual-Commit Strategy

**`manual_commit_workflow_test.go`** — the main comprehensive test:
- **`TestShadow_FullWorkflow`** — Full end-to-end: checkpoints, rewind, user commits trigger condensation, verifies `Entire-Checkpoint` trailer on commits, verifies metadata on `entire/sessions` branch, verifies sharded checkpoint paths
- **`TestShadow_TranscriptCondensation`** — Verifies transcript files (`full.jsonl`, `content_hash.txt`, `metadata.json`) end up on `entire/sessions` after condensation
- **`TestShadow_FullTranscriptContext`** — Verifies full transcript is preserved across multiple commits
- **`TestShadow_RewindAndCondensation`** — Rewind + commit = condensation only includes prompts up to rewind point
- **`TestShadow_IntermediateCommitsWithoutPrompts`** — Commits without new Claude work do NOT get trailers
- **`TestShadow_FullTranscriptCondensationWithIntermediateCommits`** — Full transcript preserved even without intermediate `SimulateUserPromptSubmit`
- **`TestShadow_TrailerRemovalSkipsCondensation`** — User removing trailer opts out of condensation
- **`TestShadow_SessionsBranchCommitTrailers`** — Verifies `Entire-Session`, `Entire-Strategy`, `Entire-Agent` trailers on `entire/sessions` branch commits
- **`TestShadow_AmendPreservesTrailer`** — `git commit --amend` preserves checkpoint trailer, no duplicate condensation

**`mid_session_commit_test.go`** — commits *during* active session (before Stop):
- **`TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript`** — Claude commits mid-session, trailer added via live transcript detection
- **`TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerWithoutTranscriptPath`** — No trailer when transcript path missing
- **`TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_NoTrailerForUnrelatedFile`** — No trailer when committed files don't overlap with Claude's work

**`phase_transitions_test.go`**:
- **`TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop`** — User commits while agent is ACTIVE: session transitions to `ACTIVE_COMMITTED`, deferred condensation fires on `TurnEnd`

**`attribution_test.go`** — attribution metadata after condensation:
- **`TestManualCommit_Attribution`** — Full attribution flow with agent + user lines
- **`TestManualCommit_AttributionDeletionOnly`** — Deletion-only commits
- **`TestManualCommit_AttributionNoDoubleCount`** — No double-counting across multiple commit cycles

### Auto-Commit Strategy

**`auto_commit_checkpoint_fix_test.go`**:
- **`TestDualStrategy_NoCheckpointForNoChanges`** — No checkpoint when prompt has no file changes
- **`TestDualStrategy_IncrementalPromptContent`** — Each checkpoint only has prompts since last checkpoint
- **`TestDualStrategy_SessionStateTracksTranscriptOffset`** — Transcript offset tracking across checkpoints

## What's NOT tested (that I can see)

The one scenario that seems like it could be a gap: **Claude making a `git commit` itself during a session via the Bash tool** (as opposed to the user committing). The `mid_session_commit_test.go` tests cover this via `GitCommitWithShadowHooks`, which simulates the hooks firing. But the specific flow where Claude runs `git commit` (which fires `prepare-commit-msg` and `post-commit` hooks) in the middle of an active turn is covered by `TestShadow_CommitBeforeStop` and `TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript`.

So yes — the existing integration tests cover this well. The critical paths are:
1. Commits get `Entire-Checkpoint` trailers when there's session work
2. Condensation copies metadata to `entire/sessions` branch
3. Mid-session commits (before Stop) work via live transcript detection
4. Deferred condensation works for `ACTIVE_COMMITTED` → `IDLE` transitions
5. Trailers are preserved on amend, skipped when removed by user

   904→// GitCommitWithShadowHooks stages and commits files, simulating the prepare-commit-msg and post-commit hooks.
   905→// This is used for testing manual-commit strategy which needs:
   906→// - prepare-commit-msg hook: adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   907→// - post-commit hook: condenses session data if trailer is present
   908→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
   909→	env.T.Helper()
   910→
   911→	// Stage files using go-git
   912→	for _, file := range files {
   913→		env.GitAdd(file)
   914→	}
   915→
   916→	// Create a temp file for the commit message (prepare-commit-msg hook modifies this)
   917→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   918→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   919→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   920→	}
   921→
   922→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook using the shared binary
   923→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile)
   924→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   925→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   926→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   927→		// Don't fail - hook may silently succeed
   928→	}
   929→
   930→	// Read the modified message
   931→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   932→	if err != nil {
   933→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   934→	}
   935→
   936→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   937→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   938→	if err != nil {
   939→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   940→	}
   941→
   942→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   943→	if err != nil {
   944→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   945→	}
   946→
   947→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   948→		Author: &object.Signature{
   949→			Name:  "Test User",
   950→			Email: "test@example.com",
   951→			When:  time.Now(),
   952→		},
   953→	})
   954→	if err != nil {
   955→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   956→	}
   957→
   958→	// Run post-commit hook using the shared binary
   959→	// This triggers condensation if the commit has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   960→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   961→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   962→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   963→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   964→		// Don't fail - hook may silently succeed
   965→	}
   966→}

   968→// GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks amends the last commit with shadow hooks.
   969→// This simulates `git commit --amend` with the prepare-commit-msg and post-commit hooks.
   970→// The prepare-commit-msg hook is called with "commit" source to indicate an amend.
   971→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
   972→	env.T.Helper()
   973→
   974→	// Stage any additional files
   975→	for _, file := range files {
   976→		env.GitAdd(file)
   977→	}
   978→
   979→	// Write commit message to temp file
   980→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   981→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   982→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   983→	}
   984→
   985→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook with "commit" source (indicates amend)
   986→	// Format: entire hooks git prepare-commit-msg <msgfile> commit
   987→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "commit")
   988→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   989→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   990→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg (amend) output: %s", output)
   991→	}
   992→
   993→	// Read the modified message
   994→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   995→	if err != nil {
   996→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   997→	}
   998→
   999→	// Amend the commit using go-git
  1000→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
  1001→	if err != nil {
  1002→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
  1003→	}
  1004→
