```
     1→// hooks_claudecode_handlers.go contains Claude Code specific hook handler implementations.
     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"encoding/json"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"log/slog"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"
    14→
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    19→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    21→)
    22→
    23→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    24→type hookInputData struct {
    25→	agent     agent.Agent
    26→	input     *agent.HookInput
    27→	sessionID string
    28→}
    29→
    30→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    31→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    32→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    33→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    39→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    45→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    46→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    47→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    48→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    49→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    50→	)
    51→
    52→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    53→	if sessionID == "" {
    54→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    58→	return &hookInputData{
    59→		agent:     ag,
    60→		input:     input,
    61→		sessionID: sessionID,
    62→	}, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    66→func captureInitialState() error {
    67→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    68→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return err
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    74→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    75→		return err
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
    79→	strat := GetStrategy()
    80→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
    81→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
    82→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
    83→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	return nil
    88→}
    89→
    90→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
    91→func commitWithMetadata() error {
    92→	// Skip on default branch for strategies that don't allow it
    93→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranchForStrategy(); skip {
    94→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping on branch '%s' - create a feature branch to use Entire tracking\n", branchName)
    95→		return nil // Don't fail the hook, just skip
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
    99→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   100→	if err != nil {
   101→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
   105→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   111→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   112→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   113→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   114→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   115→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   116→	)
   117→
   118→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   119→	if sessionID == "" {
   120→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   124→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   129→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   130→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   131→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   132→	if err != nil {
   133→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   134→	}
   135→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   136→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   140→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   141→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   142→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   143→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   144→
   145→	// Copy transcript
   146→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   147→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   148→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   149→	}
   150→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   151→
   152→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   153→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   154→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   155→	if err != nil {
   156→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   160→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   161→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   162→	var transcriptOffset int
   163→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   164→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   165→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   166→	} else {
   167→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   168→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   169→		if loadErr != nil {
   170→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   171→		}
   172→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   173→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   174→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   175→		}
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   179→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   180→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   181→	var totalLines int
   182→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   183→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   184→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   185→		if err != nil {
   186→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   187→		}
   188→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   189→	} else {
   190→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   191→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   192→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   193→		if err != nil {
   194→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   199→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   200→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   201→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   202→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   203→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   204→	}
   205→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   206→
   207→	// Extract summary
   208→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   209→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   210→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   211→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   212→	}
   213→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   214→
   215→	// Get modified files from transcript
   216→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   217→
   218→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   219→	lastPrompt := ""
   220→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   221→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   222→	}
   223→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   224→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   225→
   226→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   227→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   228→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   229→	if err != nil {
   230→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	if preState != nil {
   234→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   238→	newFiles, deletedFiles, err := ComputeFileChanges(preState)
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   244→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   245→	relNewFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(newFiles, repoRoot)
   246→	relDeletedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(deletedFiles, repoRoot)
   247→
   248→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   249→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   250→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   251→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   252→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   253→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   254→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   255→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   256→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   257→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   258→		}
   259→		return nil
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   263→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   264→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   265→	}
   266→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   267→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   268→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   269→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   270→		}
   271→	}
   272→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   273→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   274→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   275→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   276→		}
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	// Create context file before saving changes
   280→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   281→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   282→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   285→
   286→	// Get git author from local/global config
   287→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   288→	if err != nil {
   289→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// Get the configured strategy
   293→	strat := GetStrategy()
   294→
   295→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (auto-installs git hook, gitignore, etc. if needed)
   296→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   297→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   301→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   302→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   303→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   307→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   308→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   309→	if preState != nil {
   310→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   311→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   315→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   316→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   317→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   318→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
   319→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   320→		if err != nil {
   321→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   322→		} else {
   323→			tokenUsage = usage
   324→		}
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   328→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   329→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   330→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   331→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   332→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   333→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   334→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   335→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   336→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   337→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   338→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   339→		AgentType:                agentType,
   340→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   341→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   342→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   343→	}
   344→
   345→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   346→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   350→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   351→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   352→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   353→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   354→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   355→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   356→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   357→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   358→		if loadErr != nil {
   359→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   360→		}
   361→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   362→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   363→		if sessionState == nil {
   364→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   365→				SessionID: sessionID,
   366→			}
   367→		}
   368→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   369→		sessionState.StepCount++
   370→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   371→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   372→		} else {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   374→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   375→		}
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   379→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE.
   380→	// For ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE, HandleTurnEnd dispatches ActionCondense.
   381→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   382→
   383→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   384→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   385→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	return nil
   389→}
   390→
   391→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   392→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   393→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   394→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   395→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	// Get agent for logging context
   401→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   402→	if err != nil {
   403→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   407→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   408→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   409→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   410→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   411→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   412→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   413→	)
   414→
   415→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   416→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   417→	if !found {
   418→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   419→		return nil
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   423→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   424→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   425→		return nil
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   429→	modified, newFiles, deleted, err := DetectChangedFiles()
   430→	if err != nil {
   431→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   432→		return nil
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   436→	if len(modified) == 0 && len(newFiles) == 0 && len(deleted) == 0 {
   437→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   438→		return nil
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	// Get git author
   442→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   445→		return nil
   446→	}
   447→
   448→	// Get the active strategy
   449→	strat := GetStrategy()
   450→
   451→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   452→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   453→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   454→		return nil
   455→	}
   456→
   457→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   458→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   459→	if sessionID == "" {
   460→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   464→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   465→
   466→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   467→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   468→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   469→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   470→	if todoContent == "" {
   471→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   472→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   473→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   474→		if todoCount > 0 {
   475→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   476→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   477→		}
   478→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   479→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   480→	}
   481→
   482→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   483→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   484→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   485→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   486→	}
   487→
   488→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   489→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   490→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   491→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   492→		ModifiedFiles:       modified,
   493→		NewFiles:            newFiles,
   494→		DeletedFiles:        deleted,
   495→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   496→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   497→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   498→		IsIncremental:       true,
   499→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   500→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   501→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   502→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   503→		AgentType:           agentType,
   504→	}
   505→
   506→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   507→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   508→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   509→		return nil
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   513→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   514→	return nil
   515→}
   516→
   517→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   518→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   519→	// Skip on default branch for strategies that don't allow it
   520→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranchForStrategy(); skip {
   521→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping on branch '%s' - create a feature branch to use Entire tracking\n", branchName)
   522→		return nil // Don't fail the hook, just skip
   523→	}
   524→
   525→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   528→	}
   529→
   530→	// Get agent for logging context
   531→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   532→	if err != nil {
   533→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   537→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   538→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   539→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   540→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   541→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   542→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   543→	)
   544→
   545→	// Log context to stdout
   546→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   547→
   548→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   549→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   550→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   551→	// in file changes.
   552→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   553→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	return nil
   557→}
   558→
   559→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   560→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   561→	// Skip on default branch for strategies that don't allow it
   562→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranchForStrategy(); skip {
   563→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping on branch '%s' - create a feature branch to use Entire tracking\n", branchName)
   564→		return nil // Don't fail the hook, just skip
   565→	}
   566→
   567→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   568→	if err != nil {
   569→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   573→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   574→
   575→	// Get agent for logging context
   576→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   577→	if err != nil {
   578→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   579→	}
   580→
   581→	// Log parsed input context
   582→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   583→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   584→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   585→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   586→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   587→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   588→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   589→	)
   590→
   591→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   592→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   593→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   594→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   595→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   596→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   597→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   598→		}
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	// Log context to stdout
   602→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   603→
   604→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   605→	var modifiedFiles []string
   606→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   607→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   608→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   609→		if err != nil {
   610→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   611→		} else {
   612→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   613→		}
   614→	} else {
   615→		// Fall back to main transcript
   616→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   617→		if err != nil {
   618→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   619→		} else {
   620→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   621→		}
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   625→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   626→	if err != nil {
   627→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   628→	}
   629→	newFiles, err := ComputeNewFilesFromTask(preState)
   630→	if err != nil {
   631→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute new files: %v\n", err)
   632→	}
   633→
   634→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   635→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   636→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   637→	if err != nil {
   638→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   639→	}
   640→
   641→	// Filter and normalize paths
   642→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   643→	relNewFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(newFiles, repoRoot)
   644→
   645→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   646→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 {
   647→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   648→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   649→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   650→		return nil
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   654→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   655→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   656→
   657→	// Get git author
   658→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   659→	if err != nil {
   660→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   661→	}
   662→
   663→	// Get the configured strategy
   664→	strat := GetStrategy()
   665→
   666→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place
   667→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   668→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   669→	}
   670→
   671→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   672→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   673→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   674→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   675→	}
   676→
   677→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   678→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   679→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   680→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   681→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   682→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   683→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   684→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   685→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   686→		DeletedFiles:           nil, // TODO: compute deleted files
   687→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   688→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   689→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   690→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   691→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   692→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   693→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   694→		AgentType:              agentType,
   695→	}
   696→
   697→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   698→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   699→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   700→	}
   701→
   702→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   703→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   704→
   705→	return nil
   706→}
   707→
   708→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   709→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   710→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   711→}
   712→
   713→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   714→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   715→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   716→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   717→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   718→	if err != nil {
   719→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   720→	}
   721→
   722→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   723→	if err != nil {
   724→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   725→	}
   726→
   727→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   728→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   729→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   730→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   731→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   732→	)
   733→
   734→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   735→		return nil // No session to update
   736→	}
   737→
   738→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   739→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   740→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   741→	}
   742→	return nil
   743→}
   744→
   745→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   746→// ACTIVE → IDLE (or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE). Best-effort: logs warnings
   747→// on failure rather than returning errors.
   748→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   749→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   750→	if loadErr != nil {
   751→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   752→		return
   753→	}
   754→	if turnState == nil {
   755→		return
   756→	}
   757→	remaining := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   758→
   759→	// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
   760→	if len(remaining) > 0 {
   761→		strat := GetStrategy()
   762→		if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   763→			if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
   764→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   765→			}
   766→		}
   767→	}
   768→
   769→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   770→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   771→	}
   772→}
   773→
   774→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   775→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   776→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   777→	if err != nil {
   778→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   779→	}
   780→	if state == nil {
   781→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   782→	}
   783→
   784→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   785→
   786→	now := time.Now()
   787→	state.EndedAt = &now
   788→
   789→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   790→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   791→	}
   792→	return nil
   793→}
   794→
   795→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   796→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   797→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   798→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   799→
   800→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   801→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   802→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   803→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   804→//
   805→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   806→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   807→//
   808→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   809→// with whatever data is available.
   810→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   811→	const (
   812→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   813→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   814→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   815→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   816→	)
   817→
   818→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   819→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   820→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   821→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   822→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   823→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   824→			)
   825→			return
   826→		}
   827→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   828→	}
   829→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   830→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   831→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   832→	)
   833→}
   834→
   835→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   836→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   837→// close to hookStartTime.
   838→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   839→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   840→	if err != nil {
   841→		return false
   842→	}
   843→	defer f.Close()
   844→
   845→	// Seek to tail
   846→	info, err := f.Stat()
   847→	if err != nil {
   848→		return false
   849→	}
   850→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   851→	if offset < 0 {
   852→		offset = 0
   853→	}
   854→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   855→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   856→		return false
   857→	}
   858→
   859→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   860→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   861→	for _, line := range lines {
   862→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   863→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   864→			continue
   865→		}
   866→
   867→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   868→		var entry struct {
   869→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   870→		}
   871→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   872→			continue
   873→		}
   874→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   875→		if err != nil {
   876→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   877→			if err != nil {
   878→				continue
   879→			}
   880→		}
   881→
   882→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   883→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   884→			return true
   885→		}
   886→	}
   887→	return false
   888→}
   889→
```

> AGENT

Found it. There are **two problems**, and the test passes because it doesn't exercise the real code path.

## Root Cause: `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` doesn't pass `source`

Look at the test helper (`testenv.go:923`):

```go
prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile)
```

It only passes `msgFile` — **no second argument for `source`**. So `source = ""`.

But when Claude runs `git commit -m "Add ruby script"`, git calls the hook with:
```sh
entire hooks git prepare-commit-msg "$1" "$2"
#                                    file  "message"
```

So `source = "message"` in real life.

## Why this matters: the switch statement

In `manual_commit_hooks.go:391-418`, the trailer is added differently based on source:

```go
switch {
case source == "message" && !isRestoringExisting:
    // THIS IS THE REAL PATH - asks user interactively via /dev/tty
    if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", ..., true) {
        return nil  // No trailer added!
    }
    message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
// ...
default:
    // THIS IS THE TEST PATH - always adds trailer, no prompt
    message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, ...)
}
```

The test always hits `default` (source is empty) → trailer is always added, no questions asked.

In real life, `source = "message"` → `askConfirmTTY` fires → opens `/dev/tty` and writes a prompt asking the user to confirm. When Claude Code is the one running `git commit`, **nobody answers the TTY prompt**. The `ReadString('\n')` blocks, the hook hangs, the Bash tool eventually times out or kills it, and `|| true` in the hook script lets `git commit` proceed with the **unmodified message** (no trailer).

## `TestShadowStrategy_MidSessionCommit_FromTranscript` has the same bug

Same issue — it uses `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` which doesn't pass source, so it never exercises the `source="message"` path either.

## Summary

| | Test | Real life (Claude commits) |
|---|---|---|
| Hook source | `""` (empty) | `"message"` |
| Code path | `default` — always adds trailer | `askConfirmTTY` — blocks on TTY |
| Result | Trailer added | Hook hangs/killed, no trailer |

The tests are green but don't cover the actual scenario. The fix needs two things:

1. **Fix the hook logic**: when `source="message"` and the session is ACTIVE (agent is working), skip the TTY prompt and just add the trailer — the agent can't answer interactive prompts.
2. **Fix the tests**: `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` should pass `source="message"` to match what Claude actually does, so this kind of gap is caught.

Want me to plan the fix?

> AGENT

You're right to expect that. Let me look at what `isRestoringExisting` actually checks — the intent is partially there but the detection misses this case.

`manual_commit_hooks.go:377-388`:

```go
isRestoringExisting := false
if !hasNewContent && reusedSession != nil {
    // Reusing LastCheckpointID from a previous condensation
    isRestoringExisting = true
} else if hasNewContent {
    for _, state := range sessionsWithContent {
        if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
            isRestoringExisting = true
            break
        }
    }
}
```

It only detects:
1. Reusing a `LastCheckpointID` from a previous condensation
2. `PendingCheckpointID` exists (set after a prior commit+condensation)

But when Claude commits mid-session (first commit, no prior condensation), neither is true — `PendingCheckpointID` is empty, there's no `LastCheckpointID`. So `isRestoringExisting = false` → hits `askConfirmTTY`.

The session phase IS available though. The `sessions` list has `Phase` on each state, and when Claude is working, the phase is `ACTIVE`. We already have:

```go
sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
```

So the missing piece is just checking: if any session is in `PhaseActive` (agent is currently working), skip the interactive prompt — the agent can't answer it. The second `case source == "message"` branch (line 411-414) even has a comment that says "non-interactive", so the intent was there. The detection just doesn't cover "agent is actively committing."

Want me to add that check — something like "if any session is ACTIVE, treat it as non-interactive"?

> DEVELOPER

I'm struggling a bit to understand this. What does "isRestoringExisting" have to do with this, can you give a bit more context?

> AGENT

Good question, let me explain the full design of this switch statement.

The `prepare-commit-msg` hook fires on **every** `git commit`. When `source` tells us how the commit was initiated, the hook decides how to present the trailer to the user:

**`source = ""` (editor flow, e.g. `git commit`):**
The user will see the message in an editor. The hook adds the trailer + a comment explaining it. The user can delete the trailer line before saving. No interactive prompt needed — the editor IS the interaction.

**`source = "message"` (e.g. `git commit -m "..."`):**
The user will NOT see an editor, so they have no chance to review/remove the trailer. The hook needs to decide: should it silently add the trailer, or ask first?

That's where `isRestoringExisting` comes in. It answers: **"Is this just re-attaching a checkpoint ID that was already condensed?"**

- `isRestoringExisting = true`: The checkpoint data already exists on `entire/sessions`. We're just putting the same ID back on a commit (e.g., `git commit --amend -m "better message"`, or splitting Claude's work across multiple commits). Safe to add silently — no new data being linked.

- `isRestoringExisting = false`: This is genuinely new session content being linked for the first time. The user might not want it linked, so → `askConfirmTTY` to ask permission.

**The gap:** The code correctly handles "user runs `git commit -m` with new content" (ask them). But it doesn't handle "**agent** runs `git commit -m` with new content" (can't ask, should just add it). Both cases have `isRestoringExisting = false`, but only the user can answer a TTY prompt.

So the fix isn't really about `isRestoringExisting` — it's about adding a third reason to skip the prompt: "a session is in ACTIVE phase, meaning the agent is the one committing."

```
	gitDir, err := GetGitDir()
	if err != nil {
		return false // Can't determine, assume not in sequence operation
	}

	// Check for rebase state directories
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")); err == nil {
		return true
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-apply")); err == nil {
		return true
	}

	// Check for cherry-pick and revert state files
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "CHERRY_PICK_HEAD")); err == nil {
		return true
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "REVERT_HEAD")); err == nil {
		return true
	}

	return false
}

// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer to the commit message with a stable checkpoint ID.
// Only adds a trailer if there's actually new session content to condense.
// The actual condensation happens in PostCommit - if the user removes the trailer,
// the commit will not be linked to the session (useful for "manual" commits).
// For amended commits, preserves the existing checkpoint ID.
//
// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow - adds trailer with explanatory comment
//   - "message": using -m or -F flag - prompts user interactively via /dev/tty
//   - "merge", "squash", "commit": skip trailer entirely (auto-generated or amend commits)
//

func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error {
	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")

	// Skip during rebase, cherry-pick, or revert operations
	// These are replaying existing commits and should not be linked to agent sessions
	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped during git sequence operation",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("source", source),
		)
		return nil
	}

	// Skip for merge, squash, and commit (amend) sources
	// These are auto-generated or reusing existing messages - not from Claude sessions
	switch source {
	case "merge", "squash", "commit":
		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped for source",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("source", source),
		)
		return nil
	}
	repo, err := OpenRepository()
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
	// We match by worktree (not BaseCommit) because the user may have made
	// intermediate commits without entering new prompts, causing HEAD to diverge
	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
		// No active sessions or error listing - silently skip (hooks must be resilient)
		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no active sessions",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("source", source),
		)
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
	}

	// Check if any session has new content to condense
	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)

	// Determine which checkpoint ID to use
	var checkpointID id.CheckpointID
	var hasNewContent bool
	var reusedSession *SessionState

	if len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
		// New content exists - will generate new checkpoint ID below
		hasNewContent = true
	} else {
		// No new content - check if any session has a LastCheckpointID to reuse
		// This handles the case where user splits Claude's work across multiple commits
		// Reuse if: LastCheckpointID exists AND (FilesTouched is empty OR files overlap)
		// - FilesTouched empty: commits made before session stop, reuse checkpoint
		// - FilesTouched populated: only reuse if files overlap (prevents unrelated commits from reusing)

		// Get current HEAD to filter sessions
		head, err := repo.Head()
		if err != nil {
			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
		}
		currentHeadHash := head.Hash().String()

		// Filter to sessions where BaseCommit matches current HEAD
		// This prevents reusing checkpoint IDs from old sessions
		// Note: BaseCommit is kept current both when new content is condensed (in the
		// condensation process) and when no new content is found (via PostCommit when
		// reusing checkpoint IDs). If none match, we don't add a trailer rather than
		// falling back to old sessions which could have stale checkpoint IDs.
		var currentSessions []*SessionState
		for _, session := range sessions {
			if session.BaseCommit == currentHeadHash {
				currentSessions = append(currentSessions, session)
			}
		}

		if len(currentSessions) == 0 {
			// No sessions match current HEAD - don't try to reuse checkpoint IDs
			// from old sessions as they may be stale
			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no sessions match current HEAD",
				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
				slog.String("source", source),
				slog.String("current_head", currentHeadHash[:7]),
				slog.Int("total_sessions", len(sessions)),
			)
			return nil
		}

		stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
		for _, session := range currentSessions {
			if !session.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() &&
				(len(session.FilesTouched) == 0 || hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, session.FilesTouched)) {
				checkpointID = session.LastCheckpointID
				reusedSession = session
				break
			}
		}
		if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
			// No new content and no previous checkpoint to reference (or staged files are unrelated)
			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no content to link",
				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
				slog.String("source", source),
				slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
				slog.Int("sessions_with_content", len(sessionsWithContent)),
			)
			return nil
		}
	}

	// Read current commit message
	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	message := string(content)

	// Get or generate checkpoint ID (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
		// Trailer already exists (e.g., amend) - keep it
		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer already exists",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("source", source),
			slog.String("existing_checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
		)
		return nil
	}

	if hasNewContent {
		// New content: generate new checkpoint ID
		cpID, err := id.Generate()
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
		}
		checkpointID = cpID
	}
	// Otherwise checkpointID is already set to LastCheckpointID from above

	// Determine agent type and last prompt from session
	agentType := DefaultAgentType // default for backward compatibility
	var lastPrompt string
	if hasNewContent && len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
		session := sessionsWithContent[0]
		if session.AgentType != "" {
			agentType = session.AgentType
		}
		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, session)
	} else if reusedSession != nil {
		// Reusing checkpoint from existing session - get agent type and prompt from that session
		if reusedSession.AgentType != "" {
			agentType = reusedSession.AgentType
		}
		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, reusedSession)
	}

	// Prepare prompt for display: collapse newlines/whitespace, then truncate (rune-safe)
	displayPrompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(lastPrompt), 80, "...")

	// Add trailer differently based on commit source
	if source == "message" {
		// Using -m or -F: ask user interactively whether to add trailer
		// (comments won't be stripped by git in this mode)

		// Build context string for interactive prompt
		var promptContext string
		if displayPrompt != "" {
			promptContext = "You have an active " + string(agentType) + " session.\nLast Prompt: " + displayPrompt
		}

		if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", promptContext, true) {
			// User declined - don't add trailer
			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: user declined trailer",
				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
				slog.String("source", source),
			)
			return nil
		}
		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
	} else {
		// Normal editor flow: add trailer with explanatory comment (will be stripped by git)
		message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, string(agentType), displayPrompt)
	}

	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer added",
		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
		slog.String("source", source),
		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
		slog.Bool("has_new_content", hasNewContent),
	)

	// Write updated message back
	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	return nil
}

// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
// Checks if the commit has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer and if so, condenses
// session data from shadow branches to entire/sessions.
// If the user removed the trailer during commit message editing, this is treated
// as a "manual" commit and no condensation happens.
//
//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")

	repo, err := OpenRepository()
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
	head, err := repo.Head()
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
	if !found {
		// No trailer - user removed it, treat as manual commit
		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no checkpoint trailer",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
		)
		return nil
	}

	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
		)
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
	}

	// Filter to sessions with new content
	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
	if len(sessionsWithContent) == 0 {
		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no new content to condense",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
```

> AGENT

`askConfirmTTY` has been there since the initial commit (`e72157d0`, Jan 3). There was never a version that unconditionally added the trailer for `source="message"`.

**However** — `askConfirmTTY` has `defaultYes = true`:

```go
func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    if err != nil {
        return defaultYes  // true → trailer IS added
    }
```

So **if `/dev/tty` can't be opened**, the trailer IS always added. That would be the case if Claude Code's Bash subprocess doesn't have a controlling terminal (e.g., in CI, or if Claude Code detaches the subprocess from the terminal).

Maybe what you were seeing before was the trailer being added because `/dev/tty` wasn't available in whatever environment you were testing. And now something about how the process is spawned changed, so `/dev/tty` IS available, the hook blocks trying to read a response, gets killed by timeout, and the commit goes through without the trailer.

Either way — the behavior depends on an environment detail (whether `/dev/tty` is available), which is fragile. The real fix should be: if any session is in `ACTIVE` phase, skip the TTY prompt and just add the trailer, since the agent is the one committing and can't answer.

> AGENT

Both changes are clean and all tests pass. Here's a summary of what was done:

**Production fix** (`manual_commit_hooks.go`):
- Added an `isAgentActive` check that loops through sessions looking for any in `ACTIVE` phase
- When `isAgentActive` is true and `source == "message"`, the TTY prompt (`askConfirmTTY`) is skipped and the trailer is added directly
- This fixes the real-world bug where Claude's `git commit -m "..."` would hang or fail because it can't respond to TTY prompts

**Test fix** (`testenv.go`):
- `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` now passes `"message"` as the source argument, matching real `git commit -m` behavior
- Previously tests took the `default` (editor) code path which always adds the trailer, masking the bug in the `source == "message"` path

> DEVELOPER

```
    32→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    33→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    34→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    35→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    38→		return defaultYes
    39→	}
    40→	defer tty.Close()
    41→
    42→	// Show context if provided
    43→	if context != "" {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Show prompt with default indicator
    48→	// Write to tty directly, not stderr, since git hooks may redirect stderr to /dev/null
    49→	var hint string
    50→	if defaultYes {
    51→		hint = "[Y/n]"
    52→	} else {
    53→		hint = "[y/N]"
    54→	}
    55→	fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s %s ", prompt, hint)
    56→
    57→	// Read response
    58→	reader := bufio.NewReader(tty)
    59→	response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return defaultYes
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	response = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(response))
    65→	switch response {
    66→	case "y", "yes":
    67→		return true
    68→	case "n", "no":
    69→		return false
    70→	default:
    71→		// Empty or invalid input - use default
    72→		return defaultYes
    73→	}
```

Here's the issue: when the agent doesn't have a TTY, `/dev/tty` fails on line 35 and `askConfirmTTY` returns `defaultYes=true` — meaning the trailer **would** be added. So if the agent truly has no TTY, the existing code should work fine already.

But then why did you see trailers missing in your real-world test? Two possibilities:

1. **The agent DOES have a TTY** — Claude Code runs in your terminal, so its child processes inherit the controlling terminal. `/dev/tty` opens successfully, the hook writes a prompt and blocks on `ReadString('\n')` at line 59. Claude Code's Bash timeout eventually kills the process, `|| true` lets the commit proceed without the trailer.

2. **The agent doesn't have a TTY** — in which case the trailer should be added and there's a different bug.

Can we check which case it is? Let me look at how the hook script invokes the CLI.

> AGENT

So here's the interesting thing. `askConfirmTTY` already handles the no-TTY case correctly:

- Line 35-38: If `/dev/tty` can't be opened → returns `defaultYes=true` → trailer gets added
- Line 59-61: If read fails → returns `defaultYes=true` → trailer gets added

If the agent truly has no TTY, **the current code (before my edit) should already add the trailer**. The fact that you saw trailers missing suggests one of two scenarios:

**Scenario A: Agent DOES have a TTY** — Claude Code runs in your terminal, so child processes inherit the controlling terminal. `/dev/tty` opens fine, the hook writes the prompt and blocks on `ReadString('\n')`. Claude Code's Bash tool timeout kills the process, `|| true` lets git proceed, but the message file was never modified → no trailer.

**Scenario B: Agent has no TTY, but something else prevents the trailer** — maybe the session state or content detection logic bails out earlier in `PrepareCommitMsg` before reaching `askConfirmTTY`.

If it's scenario A, then checking "is TTY available" won't help because it IS available — the agent just can't respond to it. The fix would be a **read timeout** on `askConfirmTTY`, which would naturally handle both agents and humans:

- No TTY → returns immediately (current behavior)
- TTY + agent → times out → returns `defaultYes=true` → trailer added
- TTY + human → human answers within timeout → their choice honored

Want me to check which scenario it actually is? We could add a quick log line in `askConfirmTTY` to see if `/dev/tty` opens, or I can look at whether there's an early exit in `PrepareCommitMsg` that could explain the missing trailers.

> AGENT

You're right — let me retrace the actual code path for the mid-session commit scenario (agent commits before Stop):

1. No shadow branch exists yet (Stop creates it)
2. Falls to `sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript` (line 802)
3. That requires `TranscriptPath` to be set (line 860) — if it's not, returns `false`
4. If `false`: `hasNewContent = false`, no `LastCheckpointID` yet → early exit at line 309

So if the live transcript detection doesn't find content (or `TranscriptPath` isn't set), the function returns before ever reaching the switch statement. My `isAgentActive` check wouldn't even be evaluated.

The fix should be higher up. If a session is ACTIVE and we're committing, we should always add the trailer — we know the agent is working. Let me look at where the right place is to short-circuit.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Now I see the full picture. There are multiple early exits before the switch:

1. **Line 280-290**: `BaseCommit != currentHeadHash` — fails on the agent's 2nd commit because HEAD moved
2. **Line 301-309**: No `LastCheckpointID` — fails on first commit if content detection doesn't work
3. **`sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript`**: Returns `false` if `TranscriptPath` not set

So the `isAgentActive` check I added at the switch level doesn't help — the code never gets there. The fix needs to be earlier: right after finding sessions, if a session is ACTIVE and there's no TTY (agent committing), skip all the content detection and just add the trailer.

> TOOL

```json
  "old_string": "// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.\n// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).\nfunc addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {",
  "new_string": "// addTrailerForAgentCommit handles the fast path when an agent is committing\n// (ACTIVE session + no TTY). Generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer\n// directly, bypassing content detection and interactive prompts.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string, state *SessionState, source string) error {\n\t// Use PendingCheckpointID if set, otherwise generate a new one\n\tvar cpID id.CheckpointID\n\tif state.PendingCheckpointID != \"\" {\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\tcpID, err = id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcpID = id.CheckpointID{} // fall through to generate\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif cpID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\tcpID, err = id.Generate()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tcontent, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure\n\t}\n\n\tmessage := string(content)\n\n\t// Don't add if trailer already exists\n\tif _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tmessage = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)\n\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"prepare-commit-msg: agent commit trailer added\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", \"manual-commit\"),\n\t\tslog.String(\"source\", source),\n\t\tslog.String(\"checkpoint_id\", cpID.String()),\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t)\n\n\treturn os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o644) //nolint:gosec // Same permissions as git\n}\n\n// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.\n// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).\nfunc addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {"
}
```

The issue is that tests also run without a TTY, so `hasTTY()` returns false in tests too, triggering the fast path for scenarios that simulate human commits.

I'll make `hasTTY` check an env var override so tests can simulate having a TTY for human-scenario tests.

```json
  "old_string": "// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.\n// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).\nfunc hasTTY() bool {\n\ttty, err := os.OpenFile(\"/dev/tty\", os.O_RDWR, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t_ = tty.Close()\n\treturn true\n}",
  "new_string": "// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.\n// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).\n// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY=1 can be set to simulate a TTY.\nfunc hasTTY() bool {\n\tif os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY\") != \"\" {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\ttty, err := os.OpenFile(\"/dev/tty\", os.O_RDWR, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t_ = tty.Close()\n\treturn true\n}"
}
```

```
    44→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    45→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    46→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    47→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    48→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    49→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    50→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    53→		return defaultYes
    54→	}

    59→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)

    61→
```

```
+// TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer tests that when an agent commits
+// (ACTIVE session + no TTY), the trailer is always added regardless of content
+// detection. This is the fast path that bypasses transcript analysis.
+func TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Parallel()
+
+	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
+
+	session := env.NewSession()
+
+	// Start session (sets phase to ACTIVE)
+	if err := env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("SimulateUserPromptSubmit failed: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Create a file and commit as agent (no TTY)
+	env.WriteFile("agent_file.txt", "created by agent")
+	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent("Agent commit", "agent_file.txt")
+
+	// Agent commits should ALWAYS get a trailer (fast path, no content detection)
+	commitHash := env.GetHeadHash()
+	checkpointID := env.GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitHash)
+	if checkpointID == "" {
+		t.Error("Agent commit during ACTIVE session should always get a checkpoint trailer")
+	} else {
+		t.Logf("Agent commit correctly got checkpoint trailer: %s", checkpointID)
+	}
+}

+// GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent is like GitCommitWithShadowHooks but simulates
+// an agent commit (no TTY). This triggers the fast path in PrepareCommitMsg that
+// skips content detection and interactive prompts for ACTIVE sessions.
+func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent(message string, files ...string) {
+	env.T.Helper()
+
+	// Stage files using go-git
+	for _, file := range files {
+		env.GitAdd(file)
+	}
+
+	// Create a temp file for the commit message
+	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
+		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook WITHOUT ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY to simulate agent (no TTY).
+	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
+	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
+	// Explicitly filter out ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY from the environment
+	prepCmd.Env = filterEnv(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY")
+	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
+	}
+
+	// Read the modified message
+	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
+	if err != nil {
+		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
+	if err != nil {
+		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
+	if err != nil {
+		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
+		Author: &object.Signature{
+			Name:  "Test User",
+			Email: "test@example.com",
+			When:  time.Now(),
+		},
+	})
+	if err != nil {
+		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// Run post-commit hook
+	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
+	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
+	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
+	}
+}
+
+// filterEnv returns a copy of env with the specified key removed.
+func filterEnv(env []string, key string) []string {
+	prefix := key + "="
+	result := make([]string, 0, len(env))
+	for _, e := range env {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, prefix) {
+			result = append(result, e)
+		}
+	}
+	return result
+}
+
 // GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks amends the last commit with shadow hooks.
 // This simulates `git commit --amend` with the prepare-commit-msg and post-commit hooks.
 // The prepare-commit-msg hook is called with "commit" source to indicate an amend.

+// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.
+// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
+// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY=1 can be set to simulate a TTY.
+func hasTTY() bool {
+	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY") != "" {
+		return true
+	}
+	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
+	if err != nil {
+		return false
+	}
+	_ = tty.Close()
+	return true
+}
+
 // askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
 // This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
 // Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
 // If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
 func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
+	// In test mode, don't try to interact with the real TTY — just use the default.
+	// ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY simulates "a human is present" for the hasTTY() check
+	// but we can't actually read from the TTY in tests.
+	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY") != "" {
+		return defaultYes
+	}
+
 	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
 	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
 	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
@@ -239,6 +261,19 @@ func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source str
 		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
 	}
 
+	// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session + no TTY), skip
+	// content detection and interactive prompts. The agent can't respond to TTY
+	// prompts and the content detection can miss mid-session work (no shadow
+	// branch yet, transcript analysis may fail). Generate a checkpoint ID and
+	// add the trailer directly.
+	if !hasTTY() {
+		for _, state := range sessions {
+			if state.Phase.IsActive() {
+				return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx, commitMsgFile, state, source)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
 	// Check if any session has new content to condense
 	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
 
@@ -894,6 +929,54 @@ func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.
 	return true, nil
 }
 
+// addTrailerForAgentCommit handles the fast path when an agent is committing
+// (ACTIVE session + no TTY). Generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer
+// directly, bypassing content detection and interactive prompts.
+func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string, state *SessionState, source string) error {
+	// Use PendingCheckpointID if set, otherwise generate a new one
+	var cpID id.CheckpointID
+	if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
+		var err error
+		cpID, err = id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID)
+		if err != nil {
+			cpID = "" // fall through to generate
+		}
+	}
+	if cpID.IsEmpty() {
+		var err error
+		cpID, err = id.Generate()
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
+		}
+	}
+
+	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
+	}
+
+	message := string(content)
+
+	// Don't add if trailer already exists
+	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
+
+	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: agent commit trailer added",
+		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
+		slog.String("source", source),
+		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
+		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
+	)
+
+	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // Same permissions as git
+		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write commit message: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
 // Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
 func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
```

> AGENT

Here's a summary of the changes:

**Production code** (`manual_commit_hooks.go`):
- `hasTTY()` — checks if `/dev/tty` is available. Returns false for agent subprocesses (no controlling terminal)
- Early fast path in `PrepareCommitMsg`: if `!hasTTY()` and any session is `ACTIVE`, calls `addTrailerForAgentCommit` which generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer directly, bypassing all content detection and TTY prompts
- `askConfirmTTY` also respects `ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY` to avoid blocking in tests

**Test infrastructure** (`testenv.go`):
- `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` now passes `source="message"` and sets `ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY=1` — simulates a human committing with `-m`
- `GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent` — new variant that does NOT set `ENTIRE_TEST_HAS_TTY`, simulating an agent commit (no TTY)

**New test** (`mid_session_commit_test.go`):
- `TestShadowStrategy_AgentCommit_AlwaysGetsTrailer` — verifies the fast path: ACTIVE session + no TTY always gets a trailer

The key design: **TTY availability is the signal**. Human at terminal → has TTY → content detection + interactive prompt. Agent subprocess → no TTY → always add trailer for ACTIVE sessions.
